### [1] [Decoupling Geometric Planning and Execution in Scalable Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26684)
*Fernando Salanova,Cristian Mahulea,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合优先级的MAPF框架，结合几何冲突预emption和去中心化局部控制，实现了大规模多智能体路径规划的高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优和有界次优MAPF求解器依赖于时间扩展模型和集中式冲突解决，这限制了在大规模或高密度场景中的可扩展性。

Method: 采用两阶段混合优先级框架：第一阶段为几何冲突预emption(GCP)，使用A*在原始图上按优先级顺序规划，通过对进入高优先级路径顶点的转移进行成本膨胀来鼓励空间绕行；第二阶段为去中心化局部控制器(DLC)，使用顶点FIFO授权队列执行几何路径，仅在必要时插入等待动作以避免冲突。

Result: 在标准基准地图上测试最多1000个智能体，方法展现出经验上近乎线性的运行时间趋势，在满足几何可行性假设的实例上达到100%成功率。在瓶颈密集型地图上，GCP减少了同步引起的等待，通常改善了SOC。

Conclusion: 该混合框架通过将几何规划与执行时冲突解耦分离，有效解决了传统MAPF求解器的可扩展性问题，在大规模场景下展现出优异的性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) requires collision-free trajectories for multiple agents on a shared graph, often with the objective of minimizing the sum-of-costs (SOC). Many optimal and bounded-suboptimal solvers rely on time-expanded models and centralized conflict resolution, which limits scalability in large or dense instances. We propose a hybrid prioritized framework that separates geometric planning from execution-time conflict resolution. In the first stage, Geometric Conflict Preemption (GCP) plans agents sequentially with A* on the original graph while inflating costs for transitions entering vertices used by higher-priority paths, encouraging spatial detours without explicit time reasoning. In the second stage, a Decentralized Local Controller (DLC) executes the geometric paths using per-vertex FIFO authorization queues and inserts wait actions only when required to avoid vertex and edge-swap conflicts. Experiments on standard benchmark maps with up to 1000 agents show that the method scales with an empirically near-linear runtime trend and attains a 100% success rate on instances satisfying the geometric feasibility assumption. On bottleneck-heavy maps, GCP reduces synchronization-induced waiting and often improves SOC on bottleneck-heavy maps

</details>


### [2] [On the Reliability Limits of LLM-Based Multi-Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26993)
*Ruicheng Ao,Siyang Gao,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM多智能体规划在决策理论上不优于集中式贝叶斯决策者；通信约束下的性能损失可由后验收敛差异表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于LLM的多智能体规划作为委托决策问题的可靠性极限，理解分布式架构相对于集中式决策的理论局限。

Method: 将LLM多智能体架构建模为有限无环决策网络，分析其信息处理与通信机制，并与集中式贝叶斯决策者进行比较理论分析。

Result: 证明在没有新外部信号时，任何委托网络在决策理论上都被具有相同信息的集中式贝叶斯决策者支配；通信与信息压缩造成的损失在适当评分规则下可表示为期望后验收敛差异，在对数损失下退化为条件互信息，在Brier分数下退化为期望平方后验误差。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了委托LLM规划的可靠性极限，为多智能体系统设计提供了理论边界。

Abstract: This technical note studies the reliability limits of LLM-based multi-agent planning as a delegated decision problem. We model the LLM-based multi-agent architecture as a finite acyclic decision network in which multiple stages process shared model-context information, communicate through language interfaces with limited capacity, and may invoke human review. We show that, without new exogenous signals, any delegated network is decision-theoretically dominated by a centralized Bayes decision maker with access to the same information. In the common-evidence regime, this implies that optimizing over multi-agent directed acyclic graphs under a finite communication budget can be recast as choosing a budget-constrained stochastic experiment on the shared signal. We also characterize the loss induced by communication and information compression. Under proper scoring rules, the gap between the centralized Bayes value and the value after communication admits an expected posterior divergence representation, which reduces to conditional mutual information under logarithmic loss and to expected squared posterior error under the Brier score. These results characterize the fundamental reliability limits of delegated LLM planning. Experiments with LLMs on a controlled problem set further demonstrate these characterizations.

</details>


### [3] [GUIDE: Guided Updates for In-context Decision Evolution in LLM-Driven Spacecraft Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27306)
*Alejandro Carrasco,Mariko Storey-Matsutani,Victor Rodriguez-Fernandez,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了GUIDE框架，通过离线反思更新自然语言决策规则手册，实现了大语言模型在航天器操作中的跨回合自适应能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的航天器操作监管方法依赖静态提示，无法在重复执行中实现性能改进，缺乏跨回合的自适应能力。

Method: 提出GUIDE非参数策略改进框架。该框架维护一个结构化、状态条件化的自然语言决策规则手册。系统包含两部分：轻量级执行模型负责实时控制；离线反思模块根据历史轨迹更新决策规则手册。通过上下文演化而非权重更新实现策略搜索。

Result: 在Kerbal Space Program Differential Games环境中的对抗性轨道拦截任务上进行了评估。结果表明，GUIDE的演化机制持续优于静态基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM智能体中的上下文演化可视为对结构化决策规则的实时策略搜索，在闭环航天器交互任务中有效实现了策略改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as supervisory agents for spacecraft operations, but existing approaches rely on static prompting and do not improve across repeated executions. We introduce \textsc{GUIDE}, a non-parametric policy improvement framework that enables cross-episode adaptation without weight updates by evolving a structured, state-conditioned playbook of natural-language decision rules. A lightweight acting model performs real-time control, while offline reflection updates the playbook from prior trajectories. Evaluated on an adversarial orbital interception task in the Kerbal Space Program Differential Games environment, GUIDE's evolution consistently outperforms static baselines. Results indicate that context evolution in LLM agents functions as policy search over structured decision rules in real-time closed-loop spacecraft interaction.

</details>


### [4] [Toward Reliable Evaluation of LLM-Based Financial Multi-Agent Systems: Taxonomy, Coordination Primacy, and Cost Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27539)
*Phat Nguyen,Thang Pham*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体金融交易系统，提出了四维分类法、协调优先假说（CPH），指出了五种常见的评估失败问题，并引入了协调盈亏平衡利差（CBS）指标和最低评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2023年以来，基于大语言模型的多智能体金融交易系统发展迅速，但该领域缺乏一个共同的框架来理解性能驱动因素或可信地评估各项声明，亟需系统性的梳理与规范。

Method: 提出了一个涵盖架构模式、协调机制、记忆架构和工具集成的四维分类法，并将其应用于12个多智能体系统和两个单智能体基线；基于此提出了“协调优先假说”（CPH）；系统梳理了五种普遍存在的评估失败；在此基础上提出了新的评估指标CBS及最低评估标准。

Result: 构建了四维分类法并应用于现有系统；提出了协调优先假说，指出智能体间协调协议设计是交易决策质量的主要驱动因素，其影响往往超过模型规模的扩展；揭示了五种评估失败可导致报告收益率符号反转；提出了协调盈亏平衡利差（CBS）指标以判断多智能体协调是否在扣除交易成本后创造真实价值。

Conclusion: 该领域目前缺乏验证CPH所需的评估基础设施，研究呼吁建立最低评估标准作为验证CPH的前提，并强调了严谨评估与协调机制设计在未来研究中的重要性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) for financial trading have grown rapidly since 2023, yet the field lacks a shared framework for understanding what drives performance or for evaluating claims credibly. This survey makes three contributions. First, we introduce a four-dimensional taxonomy, covering architecture pattern, coordination mechanism, memory architecture, and tool integration; applied to 12 multi-agent systems and two single-agent baselines. Second, we formulate the Coordination Primacy Hypothesis (CPH): inter-agent coordination protocol design is a primary driver of trading decision quality, often exerting greater influence than model scaling. CPH is presented as a falsifiable research hypothesis supported by tiered structural evidence rather than as an empirically validated conclusion; its definitive validation requires evaluation infrastructure that does not yet exist in the field. Third, we document five pervasive evaluation failures (look-ahead bias, survivorship bias, backtesting overfitting, transaction cost neglect, and regime-shift blindness) and show that these can reverse the sign of reported returns. Building on the CPH and the evaluation critique, we introduce the Coordination Breakeven Spread (CBS), a metric for determining whether multi-agent coordination adds genuine value net of transaction costs, and propose minimum evaluation standards as prerequisites for validating the CPH.

</details>


### [5] [Sci-Mind: Cognitively-Inspired Adversarial Debate for Autonomous Mathematical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27584)
*Ruiying Sun,Wenjing Wang,Qinhan Chen,Yanhui Song,Huangwei Chen,Haotong Luan,Junhao Jia*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sci-Mind框架，通过结合经验记忆检索、对抗性认知辩证和自验证执行策略，使自主智能体能够模拟人类的科学发现过程，显著提升了数学建模的严谨性和代码可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的数学建模本质上是一种依赖经验和协作的过程。现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的自主智能体主要依赖孤立推理范式，由于缺乏领域基础和对抗性验证，经常生成看似合理但存在根本缺陷的模型。

Method: 提出Sci-Mind框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 经验记忆检索，用于检索可执行代码片段和建模范式描述，将抽象推理建立在历史解决方案基础上；(2) 对抗性认知辩证，通过优化数学连贯性的“理论家”和执行数据可行性的“实用主义者”之间的辩论，修剪优雅但不可行的公式；(3) 自验证执行策略，在代码生成前通过形式谓词确保蓝图一致性，实现完全自主执行。

Result: 在MM-Bench和EngiBench基准上的广泛实验表明，Sci-Mind在建模严谨性和代码可执行性两方面均显著优于现有的领先自主智能体。

Conclusion: Sci-Mind通过模拟人类科学发现过程中的经验借鉴和同行评审机制，有效克服了现有LLM智能体在数学建模中缺乏领域基础和验证的问题，为构建更可靠的自主建模系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Real-world mathematical modeling is inherently an experiential and collaborative endeavor. Domain experts rarely solve complex problems from scratch; instead, they draw upon analogies from historical cases and subject their hypotheses to rigorous peer scrutiny. However, autonomous agents powered by Large Language Models predominantly rely on isolated reasoning paradigms, frequently generating plausible but fundamentally flawed models due to a lack of domain grounding and adversarial verification. To address these limitations, we propose Sci-Mind, a novel framework that mirrors the human scientific discovery process. Sci-Mind integrates Experiential Memory Recall to retrieve executable code snippets and modeling paradigm descriptors, grounding abstract reasoning in historical solutions. Subsequently, it employs an Adversarial Cognitive Dialectic where a Theorist optimizing mathematical coherence and a Pragmatist enforcing data feasibility debate through competing objectives to prune elegant but infeasible formulations. A Self-Validating Execution Strategy further ensures blueprint consistency through formal predicates before code generation, achieving fully autonomous execution. Extensive experiments on the MM-Bench and EngiBench benchmarks demonstrate that Sci-Mind significantly outperforms leading autonomous agents in both modeling rigorousness and code executability.

</details>


### [6] [Emergent Social Intelligence Risks in Generative Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27771)
*Yue Huang,Yu Jiang,Wenjie Wang,Haomin Zhuang,Xiaonan Luo,Yuchen Ma,Zhangchen Xu,Zichen Chen,Nuno Moniz,Zinan Lin,Pin-Yu Chen,Nitesh V Chawla,Nouha Dziri,Huan Sun,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统分析了由大型生成模型构成的多智能体系统中的涌现性风险，发现这类系统在资源竞争、序贯协作等场景下会自发产生类似人类社会的共谋协调和从众行为等失效模式，且现有单智能体安全防护措施无法有效预防此类群体层面的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于大型生成模型的多智能体系统从实验室原型快速转向实际部署，它们在协同规划、谈判和资源分配方面展现出前所未有的可扩展性和自主性。然而，这些系统的集体交互会产生无法归因于单个智能体的失效模式，理解这些涌现性风险对于确保系统安全至关重要，现有研究尚未充分关注这一新兴问题。

Method: 本研究对涉及共享资源竞争（如计算资源或市场份额）、序贯交接协作（下游智能体仅能看到上游输出）、集体决策聚合等多种交互场景进行了先驱性研究。研究者通过大量重复实验，在不同交互条件下系统性地观察和分析了多智能体系统的群体行为模式，并测试了现有智能体级安全防护措施的有效性。

Result: 研究发现，群体性风险行为在重复实验和广泛的交互条件下频繁出现，而非罕见或病态案例。特别地，在现实的资源约束、通信协议和角色分配条件下，类似共谋的协调行为和从众行为以非平凡的频率涌现，这些现象自发地再现了人类社会中已知的病理行为模式，尽管系统并未被明确指示这样做。更重要的是，这些风险无法仅通过现有的智能体级安全防护措施来预防。

Conclusion: 这些发现揭示了智能多智能体系统的阴暗面：存在一种社会智能风险，即智能体集体在未收到相关指令的情况下，会自发复现人类社会中熟悉的失效模式。这突显了需要开发新的群体层面的安全机制来应对多智能体系统特有的涌现性风险，单纯依赖个体层面的防护已不足以保障系统安全。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems composed of large generative models are rapidly moving from laboratory prototypes to real-world deployments, where they jointly plan, negotiate, and allocate shared resources to solve complex tasks. While such systems promise unprecedented scalability and autonomy, their collective interaction also gives rise to failure modes that cannot be reduced to individual agents. Understanding these emergent risks is therefore critical. Here, we present a pioneer study of such emergent multi-agent risk in workflows that involve competition over shared resources (e.g., computing resources or market share), sequential handoff collaboration (where downstream agents see only predecessor outputs), collective decision aggregation, and others. Across these settings, we observe that such group behaviors arise frequently across repeated trials and a wide range of interaction conditions, rather than as rare or pathological cases. In particular, phenomena such as collusion-like coordination and conformity emerge with non-trivial frequency under realistic resource constraints, communication protocols, and role assignments, mirroring well-known pathologies in human societies despite no explicit instruction. Moreover, these risks cannot be prevented by existing agent-level safeguards alone. These findings expose the dark side of intelligent multi-agent systems: a social intelligence risk where agent collectives, despite no instruction to do so, spontaneously reproduce familiar failure patterns from human societies.

</details>

### [7] [A Density-Delay Law for Stable Event-Driven State Progression in Open Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26758)
*Bin Chen,Dechuang Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文推导出了分布式系统中互斥状态推进的密度-延迟定律：为了在网络规模增长时保持分叉深度有界，提案密度与传播延迟的乘积必须保持O(1)，这导致了单位层面的反比缩放定律g(N)=O(1/N)。该结果为比特币难度调整机制提供了基于缩放的理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放分布式系统中，网络延迟导致冲突窗口的存在，重叠提案可能产生竞争分支。作者旨在理解在事件驱动（而非轮次驱动）的全局状态推进过程中，如何维持系统稳定性，以及参与节点数量增长时系统需要满足的约束条件。

Method: 采用概率模型分析方法：将重叠过程近似为泊松模型，将分叉深度建模为生灭过程。在独立提案到达和有界传播延迟的假设下，推导密度-延迟乘积与分叉深度的关系，并通过不同网络规模和传播延迟下的仿真实验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论分析表明，要在参与者数量增长时维持有界的分叉深度，密度-延迟乘积λΔ必须保持O(1)，意味着总提案强度必须有界，且在单位层面上满足反比缩放定律g(N)=O(1/N)。仿真实验表明不同网络规模和传播延迟下的结果与共同的密度-延迟曲线一致，支持了预测的缩放行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为开放分布式系统中稳定的事件驱动状态推进提供了一个简洁的定律，并为比特币式的难度调整机制提供了基于缩放的解释：这是一种调节有效事件密度的去中心化方式，用于维持密度-延迟乘积在稳定范围内。

Abstract: Distributed systems in which concurrent proposals are mutually exclusive face a fundamental stability constraint under network delay. In open systems where global state progression is event-driven rather than round-driven, propagation delay creates a conflict window within which overlapping proposals may generate competing branches. This paper derives a density-delay law for such exclusive state progression processes. Under independent proposal arrivals and bounded propagation delay, overlap is approximated by a Poisson model and fork depth is represented by a birth-death process. The analysis shows that maintaining bounded fork depth as the number of participants grows requires the density-delay product $λΔ$ to remain $O(1)$, implying that aggregate proposal intensity must stay bounded and yielding an inverse-scaling law $g(N)=O(1/N)$ at the unit level. Simulation experiments across varying network sizes and propagation delays align with a common density-delay curve, supporting the predicted scaling behavior. The result provides a compact law for stable event-driven state progression in open distributed systems and offers a scaling-based interpretation of Bitcoin-style difficulty adjustment as a decentralized way to regulate effective event density.

</details>


### [8] [Fast Topology-Aware Lossy Data Compression with Full Preservation of Critical Points and Local Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26968)
*Alex Fallin,Nathaniel Gorski,Tripti Agarwal,Bei Wang,Ganesh Gopalakrishnan,Martin Burtscher*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种局部保序压缩器，能够在保持高压缩比和快速执行的同时，完全保留数据的局部顺序和所有关键点，适用于大规模浮点数据压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有损压缩算法虽然能提供足够的压缩比，但大多只保证逐点误差界限，无法保留数据的拓扑特征（如邻近点的相对大小）；而现有的拓扑保持压缩器通常仅保留部分关键点且运行速度慢，难以满足高性能科学计算的需求。

Method: 提出Local-Order-Preserving Compressor，通过保留完整的局部顺序（从而保留所有关键点）来实现拓扑保持压缩，设计了可在CPU和GPU上运行的并行算法。

Result: 该压缩器比之前的拓扑保持压缩器快数个数量级，压缩比高于无损压缩器，并且能够在CPU和GPU上产生位对位完全相同的输出，同时保证完全保留局部顺序和所有关键点。

Conclusion: Local-Order-Preserving Compressor成功解决了现有压缩方法在拓扑保持、压缩效率和执行速度之间的权衡问题，为科学计算中的大规模浮点数据压缩提供了一个高效、可移植且拓扑完整的解决方案。

Abstract: Many scientific codes and instruments generate large amounts of floating-point data at high rates that must be compressed before they can be stored. Typically, only lossy compression algorithms deliver high-enough compression ratios. However, many of them provide only point-wise error bounds and do not preserve topological aspects of the data such as the relative magnitude of neighboring points. Even topology-preserving compressors tend to merely preserve some critical points and are generally slow. Our Local-Order-Preserving Compressor is the first to preserve the full local order (and thus all critical points), runs orders of magnitude faster than prior topology-preserving compressors, yields higher compression ratios than lossless compressors, and produces bit-for-bit the same output on CPUs and GPUs.

</details>


### [9] [TX-Digital Twin: Visualizing Supercomputer GPU Performance Data Stream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27125)
*Elena Baskakova,William Bergeron,Matthew Hubbell,Hayden Jananthan,Jeremy Kepner*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了TX-Digital Twin系统，通过优化绘制调用技术，在保持性能影响最小的情况下，为超级计算机的GPU加速资源增加了内存使用、温度和功耗等关键指标的3D可视化监控功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU加速计算在超级计算机中的广泛应用，对GPU加速资源的监控和维护需求日益增长。现有的超级计算机监控系统需要处理海量的系统和性能数据，而数据可视化技术能够以易于解释的方式监督这些大规模数据流。

Method: 在MIT林肯实验室超级计算中心(LLSC)开发的TX-Digital Twin基础上进行扩展，该系统是一个结合3D游戏技术和复合数据流的超级计算机3D仿真系统。通过绘制调用优化技术，将GPU内存使用、温度和功耗等新增GPU指标整合到可视化系统中，确保在添加清晰有效的指标显示的同时，对系统性能的影响保持在最小。

Result: 成功将GPU相关指标（包括GPU内存使用、温度和功耗）集成到TX-Digital Twin系统中，实现了对这些关键性能指标的有效可视化监控，同时通过优化技术确保了系统性能不受显著影响。

Conclusion: 该扩展方案证明了将GPU监控指标有效集成到现有超级计算机数字孪生系统的可行性，为GPU加速超级计算机资源的监控和维护提供了有效的可视化解决方案。

Abstract: Supercomputers are complex, dynamic systems that serve thousands of users and are built with thousands of compute nodes. Due to the vast amounts of system and performance data needed to accurately capture their status, supercomputers require complex methods to monitor, maintain, and optimize. Data visualization is a powerful technique for overseeing these large streams of data in an easily interpretable way. The MIT Lincoln Laboratory Supercomputing Center (LLSC) enables effective monitoring through combining 3D gaming technology with compound data streams in the TX-Digital Twin, a 3D simulation of the supercomputer. The TX-Digital Twin offers both live and historical data, in visual and text formats, and tracks a multitude of revealing performance metrics.
  Recent increasing interest in GPU-accelerated computing has driven a need for monitoring and maintenance of GPU-accelerated resources in supercomputers. In this paper, we build on our previous solution by integrating the visualization of additional GPU metrics, such as GPU memory usage, temperature, and power draw, into the TX-Digital Twin. Using techniques in draw call optimization, we add clear and effective displays of the new metrics while keeping the effects on performance minimal.

</details>


### [10] [CarbonEdge: Carbon-Aware Deep Learning Inference Framework for Sustainable Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27420)
*Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo,Ziqi Tan,Hailong Jiang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了CarbonEdge，一个碳感知的深度学习推理框架，通过扩展自适应模型分区和引入碳足迹估计与绿色调度能力，在异构边缘环境中实现了22.9%的碳排放减少和1.3倍的碳效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的深度学习应用导致了AI相关碳排放的显著增长，构成了关键的可持续性挑战。现有的边缘计算框架主要优化延迟和吞吐量，但在很大程度上忽略了推理工作负载对环境的影响。

Method: 提出了CarbonEdge框架，该框架扩展了自适应模型分区技术，集成了碳足迹估计和绿色调度能力。核心是提出了一种碳感知调度算法，通过在传统加权评分中引入碳效率指标，支持可调节的性能-碳权衡（通过权重扫描实现）。

Result: 在Docker模拟的异构边缘环境实验中，CarbonEdge绿色模式相比单体执行实现了22.9%的碳排放减少。框架在碳效率上实现了1.3倍的提升（每克CO2可完成245.8次推理 vs 189.5次），且调度开销可忽略不计（每个任务0.03毫秒）。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了在可持续边缘AI部署方面的潜力，为研究人员和实践者提供了一个量化和最小化分布式深度学习推理环境足迹的工具。

Abstract: Deep learning applications at the network edge lead to a significant growth in AI-related carbon emissions, presenting a critical sustainability challenge. The existing edge computing frameworks optimize for latency and throughput, but they largely ignore the environmental impact of inference workloads. This paper introduces CarbonEdge, a carbon-aware deep learning inference framework that extends adaptive model partitioning with carbon footprint estimation and green scheduling apabilities. We propose a carbon-aware scheduling algorithm that extends traditional weighted scoring with a carbon efficiency metric, supporting a tunable performance--carbon trade-off (demonstrated via weight sweep). Experimental evaluations on Docker-simulated heterogeneous edge environments show that CarbonEdge-Green mode achieves a 22.9% reduction in carbon emissions compared to monolithic execution. The framework achieves 1.3x improvement in carbon efficiency (245.8 vs 189.5 inferences per gram CO2) with negligible scheduling overhead (0.03ms per task). These results highlight the framework's potential for sustainable edge AI deployment, providing researchers and practitioners a tool to quantify and minimize the environmental footprint of distributed deep learning inference.

</details>


### [11] [The First OpenFOAM HPC Challenge (OHC-1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27565)
*Sergey Lesnik,Gregor Olenik,Mark Wassermann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文报告了首届OpenFOAM高性能计算挑战赛（OHC-1）的结果，通过对比硬件赛道（175个数据点，25种CPU型号）和软件赛道（62个数据点，优化求解器/GPU移植）在occDrivAer算例上的表现，发现片上高带宽内存主导单节点性能，软件优化可带来高达72%的迭代时间缩短和28%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了获取OpenFOAM在当代生产硬件上的计算性能快照，并对比硬件受限提交与软件轨道优化之间的性能差异，OpenFOAM HPC技术委员会组织了此次挑战赛。

Method: 参与者运行通用的不可压缩稳态RANS算例（open-closed cooling DrivAer配置），在规定网格上进行测试。提交分为硬件赛道（参考设置）和软件赛道（修改求解器、分解策略或加速器卸载）。共收集了12个贡献者提交的237个有效数据点。

Result: 硬件赛道覆盖AMD、Intel和ARM家族的25种不同CPU型号，运行配置从单节点到256节点（32768个CPU核心），壁钟时间从7.8分钟到65.7小时，能耗从2.1到236.9 kWh。分析确定了时间和能耗之间的帕累托最优前沿，发现片上高带宽内存主导下一代CPU的单节点性能。软件赛道实现了比最佳硬件赛道结果低28%的单次迭代能耗、高17%的节点峰值性能和短72%的最小迭代时间。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了硬件和软件优化对OpenFOAM性能的影响，片上高带宽内存对单节点性能至关重要，完全GPU移植和选择性内存优化在性能提升方面领先。该手稿描述了挑战赛组织、案例设置和指标，并提出了未来挑战的展望。

Abstract: The first OpenFOAM HPC Challenge (OHC-1) was organised by the OpenFOAM HPC Technical Committee (HPCTC) to collect a snapshot of OpenFOAM's computational performance on contemporary production hardware and to compare hardware-constrained submissions with software-track optimisations. Participants ran a common incompressible steady-state RANS case, the open-closed cooling DrivAer (occDrivAer) configuration, on prescribed meshes, submitting either with the reference setup (hardware track) or with modified solvers, decomposition strategies, or accelerator offloading (software track). In total, 237 valid datapoints were submitted by 12 contributors: 175 in the hardware track and 62 in the software track. The hardware track covered 25 distinct CPU models across AMD, Intel, and ARM families, with runs spanning from single-node configurations up to 256 nodes (32768 CPU cores). Wall-clock times ranged from 7.8 minutes to 65.7 hours and reported energy-to-solution from 2.1 to 236.9 kWh. Analysis of the hardware track identified a Pareto front of optimal balance between time- and energy-to-solution, and revealed that on-package high-bandwidth memory (HBM) dominates single-node performance for next-generation CPUs. Software-track submissions achieved up to 28% lower energy per iteration, 17% higher maximum performance per node, and 72% shorter minimum time per iteration than the best hardware-track results, with full GPU ports and selective-memory optimisations leading the performance range. This manuscript describes the challenge organisation, the case setup and metrics, and presents the main findings from both tracks together with an outlook for future challenges.

</details>


### [12] [Beating vDSP: A 138 GFLOPS Radix-8 Stockham FFT on Apple Silicon via Two-Tier Register-Threadgroup Memory Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27569)
*Mohamed Amine Bergach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对Apple Silicon GPU优化的FFT实现，在N=4096复数单精度变换上达到138.45 GFLOPS，比Apple高度优化的vDSP基线提升29%。核心发现包括：threadgroup内存屏障开销极低（约2周期），而分散访问模式才是真正瓶颈；simdgroup_matrix硬件MMA首次被研究用于FFT蝶形计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon GPU缺乏公开的性能优化文档，现有FFT实现（如vDSP）仍有优化空间。作者旨在通过建立Apple GPU的两层本地内存模型（208 KiB寄存器文件+32 KiB线程组内存），探索硬件特性并榨取更高性能。

Method: 1) 基于Apple GPU硬件特性建立两层本地内存模型；2) 使用Metal着色语言实现radix-4和radix-8 split-radix Stockham内核；3) 首次研究Apple simdgroup_matrix 8×8硬件MMA用于蝶形计算；4) 对超过32 KiB线程组内存限制的尺寸采用四步分解法；5) 系统性评估内存屏障开销与访问模式影响。

Result: radix-8按时间抽取蝶形配合512线程达到最佳性能；N=4096时达138.45 GFLOPS，相比vDSP的107 GFLOPS提升29%；发现threadgroup内存屏障开销极低（约2周期），分散访问才是瓶颈；支持N=256至N=16384多尺寸变换；所有内核经vDSP验证正确。

Conclusion: Apple GPU的寄存器文件适合作为主要数据驻留层，线程组内存更适合作为交换层。simdgroup_matrix硬件MMA在FFT中的应用具有研究价值但可能非最优路径。本研究填补了Apple GPU FFT优化知识空白，为类似架构优化提供了方法论参考。

Abstract: We present an optimized Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) implementation for Apple Silicon GPUs, achieving 138.45~GFLOPS for $N\!=\!4096$ complex single-precision transforms -- a 29\% improvement over Apple's highly optimized vDSP/Accelerate baseline (107~GFLOPS). Our approach is grounded in a \emph{two-tier local memory model} that formally characterizes the Apple GPU's 208~KiB register file as the primary data-resident tier and the 32~KiB threadgroup memory as an exchange-only tier, extending the decomposition framework established in a 2015 PhD thesis on Intel integrated GPU FFT for radar processing. We implement and evaluate radix-4 and radix-8 split-radix Stockham kernels in Metal Shading Language (MSL), demonstrating that the radix-8 decimation-in-time butterfly with 512 threads yields the best performance. We further present the first investigation of Apple's \texttt{simdgroup\_matrix} 8$\times$8 hardware MMA for FFT butterfly computation and report the counter-intuitive finding that on Apple GPU, threadgroup memory barriers are inexpensive ($\sim$2 cycles) while scattered threadgroup access patterns are the true bottleneck. Our multi-size implementation supports $N\!=\!256$ through $N\!=\!16384$ using a four-step decomposition for sizes exceeding the 32~KiB threadgroup memory limit. All kernels are validated against vDSP reference outputs.

</details>


### [13] [Optimising Blockchain Scalability for Real-Time IoT Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27643)
*Hasan Mahmud Rhidoy,Mahdi H. Miraz,Iftekhar Salam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向实时物联网应用的区块链可扩展性解决方案，涵盖Layer 1增强、Layer 2链下处理、分片、边缘/雾计算集成及混合共识机制等，分析了各方案的性能优势与权衡，并指出了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链框架存在固有的可扩展性限制、吞吐量有限和高延迟问题，这与工业自动化、智能医疗和智能交通等物联网应用对超低延迟、高交易吞吐量、轻量级计算和资源高效利用的严格实时需求相冲突。

Method: 对专门适配区块链物联网的最先进可扩展性解决方案进行全面、结构化的分析，讨论包括Layer 1增强、Layer 2链下处理、基于分片的并行化、边缘和雾计算集成以及混合共识机制。对每种方法，分析其操作原理、性能收益、去中心化和安全性权衡，以及对延迟敏感部署的适用性。同时考察实时服务质量考量，包括系统响应性、能效和数据完整性。

Result: 识别出了关键的开放挑战，包括可扩展性与安全性权衡、隐私保护、互操作性和可持续资源管理，这些是大规模采用区块链物联网的持续障碍。

Conclusion: 提出了未来研究方向，强调自适应和AI驱动的共识算法、抗量子密码模型、区块链与5G/6G网络的融合以及边缘智能，为开发可扩展、安全和可持续的实时物联网区块链架构提供参考。

Abstract: The convergence of blockchain and the Internet of Things (IoT) enables secure, decentralised, and verifiable data exchange across distributed smart environments. However, traditional blockchain frameworks suffer from inherent scalability constraints, limited throughput, and high latency, which conflict with the stringent real-time requirements of IoT applications such as industrial automation, intelligent healthcare, and smart transportation. These systems demand ultra-low latency, high transaction throughput, lightweight computation, and efficient resource utilisation. This review provides a comprehensive, structured analysis of state-of-the-art scalability solutions specifically adapted to blockchain-enabled IoT. The discussion encompasses Layer 1 enhancements, Layer 2 off-chain processing, sharding-based parallelisation, integration of edge and fog computing, and hybrid consensus mechanisms. For each approach, the review highlights operational principles, performance benefits, trade-offs in decentralisation and security, and suitability for latency-sensitive deployments. Furthermore, real-time quality-of-service considerations are examined to understand how scalability strategies impact system responsiveness, energy efficiency, and data integrity. Key open challenges, including the scalability-security trade-off, privacy preservation, interoperability, and sustainable resource management, have been identified as persistent barriers to large-scale adoption. Finally, the review outlines future research directions, emphasising adaptive and AI-driven consensus algorithms, quantum-safe cryptographic models, the convergence of blockchain with 5G/6G networks, and edge intelligence. By consolidating diverse technical insights and emerging trends, this work serves as a timely reference for developing scalable, secure, and sustainable blockchain architectures for real-time IoT applications.

</details>


### [14] [jaxsgp4: GPU-accelerated mega-constellation propagation with batch parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27830)
*Charlotte Priestley,Will Handley*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文推出了jaxsgp4，一个基于JAX的高性能SGP4轨道传播开源实现，通过GPU并行化实现了对Starlink星座（9341颗卫星）的极速传播，相比传统C++基线加速1500倍，为大规模空间物体碰撞预警和态势感知提供了高效解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人造空间物体数量从稀疏集群向超过10万颗卫星的巨型星座演进，传统CPU实现的SGP4算法在碰撞规避和空间态势感知任务中面临计算瓶颈，难以满足大规模处理需求。

Method: 利用JAX库重构SGP4算法为纯函数式范式，借助即时编译(JIT)、自动向量化以及自动优化功能，在现代GPU上实现大规模并行轨道传播；并采用32位精度以在硬件加速器上获得更高的吞吐量。

Result: jaxsgp4在单块A100 GPU上能在4毫秒内将整个Starlink星座（9341颗卫星）各传播至1000个未来时间步，相比传统C++基线实现约1500倍加速；使用32位精度在牺牲极小精度损失的前提下显著提升了吞吐量。

Conclusion: jaxsgp4为海量空间物体的轨道传播提供了一个高效、可扩展的开源工具，能够满足未来巨型星座时代对碰撞预警和空间态势感知的高性能计算需求，证明了现代硬件加速和函数式编程范式在天体力学计算中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: As the population of anthropogenic space objects transitions from sparse clusters to mega-constellations exceeding 100,000 satellites, traditional orbital propagation techniques face a critical bottleneck. Standard CPU-bound implementations of the Simplified General Perturbations 4 (SGP4) algorithm are less well suited to handle the requisite scale of collision avoidance and Space Situational Awareness (SSA) tasks. This paper introduces \texttt{jaxsgp4}, an open-source high-performance reimplementation of SGP4 utilising the \texttt{JAX} library. \texttt{JAX} has gained traction in the landscape of computational research, offering an easy mechanism for Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation, automatic vectorisation and automatic optimisation of code for CPU, GPU and TPU hardware modalities. By refactoring the algorithm into a pure functional paradigm, we leverage these transformations to execute massively parallel propagations on modern GPUs. We demonstrate that \texttt{jaxsgp4} can propagate the entire Starlink constellation (9,341 satellites) each to 1,000 future time steps in under 4 ms on a single A100 GPU, representing a speedup of $1500\times$ over traditional C++ baselines. Furthermore, we argue that the use of 32-bit precision for SGP4 propagation tasks offers a principled trade-off, sacrificing negligible precision loss for a substantial gain in throughput on hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [15] [Operational Strategies for Non-Disruptive Scheduling Transitions in Production HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27863)
*Glen MacLachlan,Joseph Creech,Rubeel Muhammad Iqbal,Clark Gaylord,Jake Messick*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种在不停机的情况下将生产环境HPC集群从中途迁移到可消耗资源调度策略的案例，通过兼容层、可观测性反馈和用户引导，成功将队列等待时间从小时级降至分钟级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将异构高性能计算（HPC）系统迁移到资源感知调度面临技术和用户行为双重挑战，尤其是在已建立用户工作流的生产环境中，直接切换会严重干扰现有研究任务，因此需要一种无中断的平滑过渡方案。

Method: 提出并实施了一种综合运营策略，包括：构建有时限的兼容层以维持旧作业提交方式、利用可观测性驱动反馈监控资源使用、以及开展针对性的用户参与和培训，引导用户采用显式资源声明（TRES）提交作业。

Result: 迁移部署后，CPU工作负载的队列等待时间中位数从277分钟降至不到3分钟，GPU工作负载从81分钟降至3.4分钟；采用TRES提交方式的用户表现出很强的长期留存率，且未中断活跃的研究工作流。

Conclusion: 成功的调度系统迁移不仅依赖于系统配置的正确性，更关键在于将可观测性、用户参与和运营设计三者有机结合，以在不干扰用户生产的前提下实现资源利用效率的显著提升。

Abstract: Migrating heterogeneous high-performance computing (HPC) systems to resource-aware scheduling introduces both technical and behavioral challenges, particularly in production environments with established user workflows. This paper presents a case study of transitioning a production academic HPC cluster from node-exclusive to consumable resource scheduling mid-lifecycle, without disrupting active workloads. We describe an operational strategy combining a time-bounded compatibility layer, observability-driven feedback, and targeted user engagement to guide adoption of explicit resource declaration. This approach protected active research workflows throughout the transition, avoiding the disruption that a direct cut-over would have imposed on the user community. Following deployment, median queue wait times fell from 277 minutes to under 3 minutes for CPU workloads and from 81 minutes to 3.4 minutes for GPU workloads. Users who adopted TRES-based submission exhibited strong long-term retention. These results demonstrate that successful scheduling transitions depend not only on system configuration, but on aligning observability, user engagement, and operational design.

</details>


### [16] [Varuna: Enabling Failure-Type Aware RDMA Failover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28001)
*Xiaoyang Wang,Yongkun Li,Lulu Yao,Guoli Wei,Longcheng Yang,Yinlong Xu,Weiqing Kong,Weiguang Wang,Peng Dong,Bingyang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Varuna，一种故障类型感知的RDMA恢复机制。通过区分链路故障前后的在途请求，仅重传未执行的请求，避免了冗余重传和非幂等操作的语义错误，实现了微秒级故障转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生产数据中心通过备用链路容忍RDMA链路故障，但在故障时会盲目重传所有在途请求。这不仅浪费带宽，对于非幂等操作还会因重复执行导致应用语义错误。因此需要一种能够区分已执行和未执行请求的精确重传机制。

Method: Varuna在每个RDMA操作上附带一个轻量级完成日志。链路故障发生后，该日志能够确定性地区分已执行的请求（故障后请求）和丢失的请求（故障前请求）。系统仅重传故障前请求子集，并为故障后请求获取/恢复返回值。

Result: 在综合微基准测试和端到端RDMA TPC-C事务测试中，Varuna在现实应用中仅引入0.6-10%的稳态延迟开销，消除了65%的恢复重传时间，保持了事务一致性，并在RDMA故障转移期间引入零连接重建开销和可忽略的内存开销。

Conclusion: Varuna成功实现了正确且高效的RDMA故障恢复，通过避免不必要的重传显著降低了恢复开销，同时保证了非幂等操作的正确性和事务一致性，为数据中心RDMA网络提供了实用的故障恢复解决方案。

Abstract: RDMA link failures can render connections temporarily unavailable, causing both performance degradation and significant recovery overhead. To tolerate such failures, production datacenters assign each primary link with a standby link and, upon failure, uniformly retransmit all in-flight RDMA request over the backup path. However, we observe that such blanket retransmission is unnecessary. In-flight requests can be split into pre-failure and post-failure categories depending on whether the responder has already executed. Retransmitting post-failure requests is not only redundant (consuming bandwidth), but also incorrect for non-idempotent operations, where duplicate execution can violate application semantics.
  We present Varuna, a failure-type-aware RDMA recovery mechanism that enables correct retransmission and us-level failover. Varuna piggybacks a lightweight completion log on every RDMA operation; after a link failure, this log deterministically reveals which in-flight requests were executed (post-failure) and which were lost (pre-failure). Varuna then retransmits only the pre-failure subset and fetches/recovers the return values for post-failure requests. Evaluated using synthetic microbenchmarks and end-to-end RDMA TPC-C transactions, Varuna incurs only 0.6-10% steady-state latency overhead in realistic applications, eliminates 65% of recovery retransmission time, preserves transactional consistency, and introduces zero connectivity rebuild overhead and negligible memory overhead during RDMA failover.

</details>


### [17] [Efficient Counting and Simulation in Content-Oblivious Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28260)
*Jérémie Chalopin,Yi-Jun Chang,Giuseppe Antonio Di Luna,Haoran Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了内容无关(CO)模型中的高效计算问题。提出了在环拓扑中进行计数的高效算法，其中匿名环需要$O(n^{1.5})$个脉冲，带ID的环需要$O(n\log^2 n)$个脉冲，并证明了计数问题的下界为$\Omega(n\log n)$。此外，设计了一个高效的模拟器，可在$O(b)$个脉冲内模拟一条$b$比特消息的发送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的内容无关模型模拟器效率极低，需要$O(n^3b+n^3\log n)$个脉冲才能模拟一条$b$比特消息，即使在小规模网络上也不实用。同时，网络规模计数是许多分布式任务的基本前提，也是聚合原语的基础。因此，本文旨在研究CO网络中消息高效的计算问题，聚焦于环拓扑的计数问题。

Method: 本文提出了一系列计数算法和模拟器：（1）针对有领导者的匿名环，提出了$O(n^{1.5})$脉冲的计数算法；（2）针对带ID的环，提出了$O(n\log^2 n)$脉冲的计数算法；（3）证明了任何CO计数算法至少需要$\Omega(n\log n)$个脉冲；（4）设计了经典消息传递的高效模拟器，每个进程只需$O(b)$个脉冲即可发送$b$比特消息到各邻居，该模拟器可扩展到一般2边连通网络。

Result: 在计数方面：匿名环算法复杂度为$O(n^{1.5})$脉冲，带ID环算法复杂度为$O(n\log^2 n)$脉冲，下界为$\Omega(n\log n)$脉冲。在模拟方面：单个$b$比特消息的发送只需$O(b)$个脉冲（对比之前$O(n^3b)$的复杂度有显著改进），扩展到一般2边连通网络需要$O(n^8\log n)$脉冲的预处理。

Conclusion: 本文显著提升了内容无关模型的计算效率，证明了在环拓扑中可以进行高效的计数操作，并设计出了线性脉冲复杂度的消息传递模拟器，为内容无关网络中的高效分布式计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the content-oblivious (CO) model (proposed by Censor-Hillel et al.), processes inhabit an asynchronous network and communicate only by exchanging pulses. A series of works has clarified the computational power of this model. In particular, it was shown that, when a leader is present and the network is 2-edge-connected, content-oblivious communication can simulate classical asynchronous message passing. Subsequent results extended this equivalence to leaderless oriented and unoriented rings, and, under non-uniform assumptions, to general 2-edge-connected networks. The simulator of Censor-Hillel et al. requires $O(n^3b+n^3\log n)$ pulses to emulate the send of a single $b$-bit message, making it impractical even on modest-size networks. We focus on message-efficient computation in CO networks. We study the fundamental problem of counting in ring topologies, both because knowing the exact network size is a basic prerequisite for many distributed tasks and because counting immediately implies a broad class of aggregation primitives. We give an algorithm that counts using $O(n^{1.5})$ pulses in anonymous rings with a leader, an $O(n\log^2 n)$ algorithm for counting in rings with IDs. Moreover, we show that any counting algorithm in CO requires $Ω(n\log n)$ pulses. Interestingly, in the course of this investigation, we design a simulator for classic message passing: in one simulated round, each process can send a $b$-bit message to each of its neighbors using only $O(b)$ pulses per process. The simulator extends to general 2-edge-connected networks, after a pre-processing step that requires $O(n^{8}\log n)$ pulses, where $n$ is the number of processes, allowing thus efficient simulation of asynchronous message passing in general 2-edge-connected networks.

</details>

*En-Ming Huang,Shih-Hao Hung*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Warp-STAR，一种通过消除warp内负载不平衡来加速GPU静态时序分析（STA）的引擎，相比现有最优GPU方案提速2.4倍，在时序驱动全局布局中提速1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态时序分析（STA）是电子设计自动化（EDA）流程的关键步骤但也是计算瓶颈；现有基于GPU的STA引擎虽快于CPU，但因电路图的不规则性导致warp内负载不平衡，效率受限。

Method: 提出Warp-STAR，一种新型GPU加速STA引擎，通过在warp层级编排并行计算，有效消除了由不规则电路图引起的warp内负载不平衡问题。

Result: Warp-STAR相比此前最先进的GPU STA引擎实现了2.4倍加速；集成至时序驱动全局布局框架后，相比最优框架实现1.7倍加速；在可微分梯度分析中也表现出有效性与极小开销。

Conclusion: Warp-STAR成功解决了GPU加速STA中的warp内负载不平衡问题，显著提升了计算效率，验证了warp级并行编排策略的有效性，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Static timing analysis (STA) is crucial for Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flows but remains a computational bottleneck. While existing GPU-based STA engines are faster than CPU, they suffer from inefficiencies, particularly intra-warp load imbalance caused by irregular circuit graphs. This paper introduces Warp-STAR, a novel GPU-accelerated STA engine that eliminates this imbalance by orchestrating parallel computations at the warp level. This approach achieves a 2.4X speedup over previous state-of-the-art (SoTA) GPU-based STA. When integrated into a timing-driven global placement framework, Warp-STAR delivers a 1.7X speedup over SoTA frameworks. The method also proves effective for differentiable gradient analysis with minimal overhead.

</details>


### [19] [Trust-Aware Routing for Distributed Generative AI Inference at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28622)
*Chanh Nguyen,Erik Elmroth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了G-TRAC框架，通过将路由问题建模为风险约束最短路径问题，并结合混合信任架构，实现了在去中心化边缘设备上对生成式AI推理的可靠、可信协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI推理逐渐在去中心化、异构的边缘设备上执行，单一节点的故障或恶意行为会破坏整个推理过程，传统的尽力而为（best-effort）P2P路由无法满足可靠性、性能可变性和信任管理的需求。

Method: 1. 将路由问题建模为“风险约束最短路径”（Risk-Bounded Shortest Path）计算，提出一种结合信任下限剪枝与Dijkstra搜索的多项式时间算法，实现毫秒级路由延迟。2. 设计“混合信任架构”（Hybrid Trust Architecture），在稳定锚点维护全局声誉状态，并通过后台同步向边缘节点分发轻量级更新，以支持动态环境下的路由逻辑。

Result: 在异构商用设备测试平台上的实验表明，G-TRAC显著提高了推理完成率，有效隔离了不可靠节点，并在节点故障和网络分区情况下维持了鲁棒的执行能力。路由延迟在实际边缘规模下中位数低于1毫秒，更大规模下仍保持在10毫秒以内。

Conclusion: G-TRAC成功地为分布式生成式AI推理提供了一种信任感知的协调机制，证明了在动态、不可靠的边缘环境中，通过算法与系统协议的协同设计，可以实现高效且鲁棒的推理执行。

Abstract: Emerging deployments of Generative AI increasingly execute inference across decentralized and heterogeneous edge devices rather than on a single trusted server. In such environments, a single device failure or misbehavior can disrupt the entire inference process, making traditional best-effort peer-to-peer routing insufficient. Coordinating distributed generative inference therefore requires mechanisms that explicitly account for reliability, performance variability, and trust among participating peers.
  In this paper, we present G-TRAC, a trust-aware coordination framework that integrates algorithmic path selection with system-level protocol design to ensure robust distributed inference. First, we formulate the routing problem as a \textit{Risk-Bounded Shortest Path} computation and introduce a polynomial-time solution that combines trust-floor pruning with Dijkstra's search, achieving sub-millisecond median routing latency at practical edge scales, and remaining below 10 ms at larger scales. Second, to operationally support the routing logic in dynamic environments, the framework employs a \textit{Hybrid Trust Architecture} that maintains global reputation state at stable anchors while disseminating lightweight updates to edge peers via background synchronization.
  Experimental evaluation on a heterogeneous testbed of commodity devices demonstrates that G-TRAC significantly improves inference completion rates, effectively isolates unreliable peers, and sustains robust execution even under node failures and network partitions.

</details>

*Xingguo Chen,Pingshou Xiong,Zhenyu Luo,Mengfei Hu,Xinwen Li,Yongzhou Lü,Guang Yang,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于比特棋盘优化和改进强化学习算法的高性能俄罗斯方块AI框架，实现了53倍加速，在3分钟内达到平均3829分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的俄罗斯方块实现存在仿真速度慢、状态评估次优和训练范式低效等问题，限制了其在大规模强化学习研究中的应用价值。

Method: 采用比特棋盘表示重新设计游戏板和方块，利用位运算加速核心过程；引入后状态评估的演员网络简化状态价值估计；提出缓冲优化的PPO算法平衡采样与更新效率；开发符合OpenAI Gym标准的Python-Java接口。

Result: 相比OpenAI Gym-Tetris实现53倍加速；后状态评估网络优于传统动作价值网络且参数更少；在10x10网格上3分钟内达到平均3829分。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合底层比特棋盘优化与高层AI策略，增强了俄罗斯方块作为强化学习基准的实用性，为可扩展的序列决策研究提供了样本高效且计算轻量的解决方案。

Abstract: The efficiency of game engines and policy optimization algorithms is crucial for training reinforcement learning (RL) agents in complex sequential decision-making tasks, such as Tetris. Existing Tetris implementations suffer from low simulation speeds, suboptimal state evaluation, and inefficient training paradigms, limiting their utility for large-scale RL research. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a high-performance Tetris AI framework based on bitboard optimization and improved RL algorithms. First, we redesign the Tetris game board and tetrominoes using bitboard representations, leveraging bitwise operations to accelerate core processes (e.g., collision detection, line clearing, and Dellacherie-Thiery Features extraction) and achieve a 53-fold speedup compared to OpenAI Gym-Tetris. Second, we introduce an afterstate-evaluating actor network that simplifies state value estimation by leveraging Tetris afterstate property, outperforming traditional action-value networks with fewer parameters. Third, we propose a buffer-optimized Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm that balances sampling and update efficiency, achieving an average score of 3,829 on 10x10 grids within 3 minutes. Additionally, we develop a Python-Java interface compliant with the OpenAI Gym standard, enabling seamless integration with modern RL frameworks. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework enhances Tetris's utility as an RL benchmark by bridging low-level bitboard optimizations with high-level AI strategies, providing a sample-efficient and computationally lightweight solution for scalable sequential decision-making research.

</details>


### [21] [Multiverse: Language-Conditioned Multi-Game Level Blending via Shared Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26782)
*In-Chang Baek,Jiyun Jung,Sung-Hyun Kim,Geum-Hwan Hwang,Kyung-Joong Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multiverse，一种基于语言条件的多游戏关卡生成器，能够通过文本指令实现跨游戏的关卡融合，显著提升了同类型游戏的融合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到关卡生成器通常局限于单一游戏领域，缺乏能够捕捉跨领域结构关系的表征，难以将语言条件的生成扩展到多个游戏中。

Method: 提出了Multiverse模型，学习一个对齐文本指令和关卡结构的共享潜在空间；引入基于阈值的多正例对比监督机制，链接跨游戏中语义相关的关卡，从而利用语言引导不同游戏内容的结构融合。

Result: 实验表明，学习到的表征支持可控的跨游戏关卡融合，在同类型游戏的融合质量上有显著提升，并能基于组合式文本提示实现零样本生成。

Conclusion: 该方法为语言条件的多游戏内容生成提供了一个统一的表征，实现了通过自然语言对跨游戏关卡融合的有效控制。

Abstract: Text-to-level generation aims to translate natural language descriptions into structured game levels, enabling intuitive control over procedural content generation. While prior text-to-level generators are typically limited to a single game domain, extending language-conditioned generation to multiple games requires learning representations that capture structural relationships across domains. We propose Multiverse, a language-conditioned multi-game level generator that enables cross-game level blending through textual specifications. The model learns a shared latent space aligning textual instructions and level structures, while a threshold-based multi-positive contrastive supervision links semantically related levels across games. This representation allows language to guide which structural characteristics should be preserved when combining content from different games, enabling controllable blending through latent interpolation and zero-shot generation from compositional textual prompts. Experiments show that the learned representation supports controllable cross-game level blending and significantly improves blending quality within the same game genre, while providing a unified representation for language-conditioned multi-game content generation.

</details>


### [22] [Concerning Uncertainty -- A Systematic Survey of Uncertainty-Aware XAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26838)
*Helena Löfström,Tuwe Löfström,Anders Hjort,Fatima Rabia Yapicioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了不确定性感知的可解释人工智能（UAXAI），总结了贝叶斯、蒙特卡洛和共形三种不确定性量化方法，归纳了评估可信度、约束模型和显式传达不确定性三种集成策略，指出当前评估实践碎片化且缺乏用户视角，并提出了统一评估原则及未来的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可解释人工智能（XAI）的发展，单纯的解释已不足以满足高风险场景的需求。为了增强解释的可靠性与鲁棒性，研究者开始将不确定性量化融入解释流程，但该领域尚缺乏系统性的梳理与统一的评估标准，因此亟需对现有的UAXAI方法进行综述并明确未来的发展方向。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统调研了不确定性感知的可解释人工智能相关文献。通过对现有工作进行分类与分析，识别出三种主要的不确定性量化方法（贝叶斯、蒙特卡洛、共形方法）以及三种将不确定性集成到解释中的策略（评估可信度、约束模型或解释、显式传达不确定性）。

Result: 研究发现，当前的评估实践呈现碎片化特征，主要聚焦于模型本身，对终端用户的关注有限，且对校准性、覆盖率和解释稳定性等可靠性属性的报告不一致。近期研究趋向于校准和无分布技术，并开始重视解释器的变异性问题。反事实解释和校准方法被视为连接可解释性与可靠性的有效途径。

Conclusion: UAXAI领域的发展需要建立统一的评估原则，将不确定性传播、模型鲁棒性与人类决策过程紧密联系起来。未来的研究应重点关注反事实方法与校准技术，以实现可解释性与可靠性的对齐，推动该领域的标准化与实用化发展。

Abstract: This paper surveys uncertainty-aware explainable artificial intelligence (UAXAI), examining how uncertainty is incorporated into explanatory pipelines and how such methods are evaluated. Across the literature, three recurring approaches to uncertainty quantification emerge (Bayesian, Monte Carlo, and Conformal methods), alongside distinct strategies for integrating uncertainty into explanations: assessing trustworthiness, constraining models or explanations, and explicitly communicating uncertainty. Evaluation practices remain fragmented and largely model centered, with limited attention to users and inconsistent reporting of reliability properties (e.g., calibration, coverage, explanation stability). Recent work leans towards calibration, distribution free techniques and recognizes explainer variability as a central concern. We argue that progress in UAXAI requires unified evaluation principles that link uncertainty propagation, robustness, and human decision-making, and highlight counterfactual and calibration approaches as promising avenues for aligning interpretability with reliability.

</details>


### [23] [Neuro-Symbolic Learning for Predictive Process Monitoring via Two-Stage Logic Tensor Networks with Rule Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26944)
*Fabrizio De Santis,Gyunam Park,Francesco Zanichelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号方法，通过Logic Networks将领域知识（如顺序约束和逻辑规则）集成到序列事件预测模型中，并采用两阶段优化策略解决了逻辑约束与预测精度之间的权衡问题，在欺诈检测和医疗监控等场景中显著提升了预测性能和合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动方法仅从历史数据中学习相关性，未能整合领域特定的序列约束和逻辑规则（如医疗程序的执行顺序、金融交易的合规规则），这限制了预测准确性和监管合规性。

Method: 提出了一种神经符号方法，利用Logic Networks（LTNs）将领域知识转化为可微分的逻辑约束。具体包括：1）使用线性时序逻辑和一阶逻辑形式化控制流、时间和负载知识；2）创新性地提出了两阶段优化策略：预训练阶段使用加权公理损失优先学习数据，随后进行规则剪枝，仅保留一致且有贡献的公理，以解决LTNs倾向于牺牲预测准确性来满足逻辑公式的问题。

Result: 在四个真实事件日志上的评估表明：注入领域知识显著提升了预测性能；两阶段优化策略被证明是关键（没有它，知识注入可能严重降低性能）。该方法在合规约束严格且合规训练样本有限的场景下表现尤为出色，在确保遵守领域约束的同时，性能优于纯数据驱动的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了将领域知识以可微分逻辑约束的形式集成到预测模型中的有效性。所提出的两阶段优化策略有效解决了知识注入过程中的性能权衡问题，为欺诈检测和医疗监控等需要高合规性和准确性的领域提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Predictive modeling on sequential event data is critical for fraud detection and healthcare monitoring. Existing data-driven approaches learn correlations from historical data but fail to incorporate domain-specific sequential constraints and logical rules governing event relationships, limiting accuracy and regulatory compliance. For example, healthcare procedures must follow specific sequences, and financial transactions must adhere to compliance rules. We present a neuro-symbolic approach integrating domain knowledge as differentiable logical constraints using Logic Networks (LTNs). We formalize control-flow, temporal, and payload knowledge using Linear Temporal Logic and first-order logic. Our key contribution is a two-stage optimization strategy addressing LTNs' tendency to satisfy logical formulas at the expense of predictive accuracy. The approach uses weighted axiom loss during pretraining to prioritize data learning, followed by rule pruning that retains only consistent, contributive axioms based on satisfaction dynamics. Evaluation on four real-world event logs shows that domain knowledge injection significantly improves predictive performance, with the two-stage optimization proving essential knowledge (without it, knowledge can severely degrade performance). The approach excels particularly in compliance-constrained scenarios with limited compliant training examples, achieving superior performance compared to purely data-driven baselines while ensuring adherence to domain constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Compliance-Aware Predictive Process Monitoring: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26948)
*Fabrizio De Santis,Gyunam Park,Wil M. P. van der Aalst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逻辑张量网络(LTNs)的神经符号预测过程监控方法，通过将过程知识注入预测模型，解决了传统纯数据驱动方法无法融合领域约束的问题，在合规性和预测准确性上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测过程监控方法属于次符号化方法，完全基于数据学习描述特征与目标特征之间的相关性，无法融合领域特定的过程约束（知识）。这种局限性导致模型难以满足合规性要求，且预测准确性受限。

Method: 提出了一种基于逻辑张量网络(LTNs)的神经符号方法，通过包含四个关键阶段的结构化管道实现：1)特征提取；2)规则提取；3)知识库创建；4)知识注入。该方法将过程知识有效融入到预测模型中。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法不仅能够学习过程约束，还在所有合规性感知实验中表现出更高的合规性和更优的预测准确性，全面超越了基线方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法成功解决了传统次符号化方法在预测过程监控中的局限性，通过注入领域知识实现了更好的合规性遵循和预测性能提升，为预测过程监控领域提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Existing approaches for predictive process monitoring are sub-symbolic, meaning that they learn correlations between descriptive features and a target feature fully based on data, e.g., predicting the surgical needs of a patient based on historical events and biometrics. However, such approaches fail to incorporate domain-specific process constraints (knowledge), e.g., surgery can only be planned if the patient was released more than a week ago, limiting the adherence to compliance and providing less accurate predictions. In this paper, we present a neuro-symbolic approach for predictive process monitoring, leveraging Logic Tensor Networks (LTNs) to inject process knowledge into predictive models. The proposed approach follows a structured pipeline consisting of four key stages: 1) feature extraction; 2) rule extraction; 3) knowledge base creation; and 4) knowledge injection. Our evaluation shows that, in addition to learning the process constraints, the neuro-symbolic model also achieves better performance, demonstrating higher compliance and improved accuracy compared to baseline approaches across all compliance-aware experiments.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have revolutionized medical reasoning tasks, yet single-agent systems often falter on complex, interdisciplinary problems requiring robust handling of uncertainty and conflicting evidence. Multi-agent systems (MAS) leveraging LLMs enable collaborative intelligence, but prevailing centralized architectures suffer from scalability bottlenecks, single points of failure, and role confusion in resource-constrained environments. Decentralized MAS (D-MAS) promise enhanced autonomy and resilience via peer-to-peer interactions, but their application to high-stakes healthcare domains remains underexplored. We introduce MediHive, a novel decentralized multi-agent framework for medical question answering that integrates a shared memory pool with iterative fusion mechanisms. MediHive deploys LLM-based agents that autonomously self-assign specialized roles, conduct initial analyses, detect divergences through conditional evidence-based debates, and locally fuse peer insights over multiple rounds to achieve consensus. Empirically, MediHive outperforms single-LLM and centralized baselines on MedQA and PubMedQA datasets, attaining accuracies of 84.3% and 78.4%, respectively. Our work advances scalable, fault-tolerant D-MAS for medical AI, addressing key limitations of centralized designs while demonstrating superior performance in reasoning-intensive tasks.

Conclusion: 合规不能简化为事后标注，透明度应被视为架构设计要求。弥合法律期望与技术现实之间的差距需要跨学科研究，整合法律语义学、AI工程和人本设计三个领域。

Using synthetic data generation and automated fact-checking as diagnostic use cases, we show that compliance cannot be reduced to post-hoc labeling. In fact-checking pipelines, provenance tracking is not feasible under iterative editorial workflows and non-deterministic LLM outputs; moreover, the assistive-function exemption does not apply, as such systems actively assign truth values rather than supporting editorial presentation. In synthetic data generation, persistent dual-mode marking is paradoxical: watermarks surviving human inspection risk being learned as spurious features during training, while marks suited for machine verification are fragile under standard data processing. Across both domains, three structural gaps obstruct compliance: (a) absent cross-platform marking formats for interleaved human-AI outputs; (b) misalignment between the regulation's 'reliability' criterion and probabilistic model behavior; and (c) missing guidance for adapting disclosures to heterogeneous user expertise. Closing these gaps requires transparency to be treated as an architectural design requirement, demanding interdisciplinary research across legal semantics, AI engineering, and human-centered desi

</details>


### [27] [EpochX: Building the Infrastructure for an Emergent Agent Civilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27304)
*Huacan Wang,Chaofa Yuan,Xialie Zhuang,Tu Hu,Shuo Zhang,Jun Han,Shi Wei,Daiqiang Li,Jingping Liu,Kunyi Wang,Zihan Yin,Zhenheng Tang,Andy Wang,Henry Peng Zou,Philip S. Yu,Sen Hu,Qizhen Lan,Ronghao Chen*

TL;DR: 论文提出了EpochX，一个面向人机协作生产网络的积分原生市场基础设施，旨在解决AI代理规模化应用中工作委托、验证和激励的组织问题，使完成的任务能产生可复用的生态系统资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用技术重塑经济的关键在于重新组织生产和协调方式，而非仅仅改进单一工具。随着基础模型使广泛任务执行和工具使用变得容易，AI代理发展的瓶颈已从原始能力转移到如何规模化地委托、验证和奖励工作。

Method: 构建了EpochX市场基础设施，将人类和代理视为对等参与者。系统允许发布和认领任务，支持任务分解、交付流程、验证和验收。核心设计包括：(1) 使完成的交易产生可复用资产（技能、工作流、执行痕迹、提炼经验），并存储显式依赖结构；(2) 引入原生积分机制，锁定任务赏金、预算委托、结算奖励，并在验证资产被复用时补偿创建者。

Result: EpochX成功形式化了端到端的交易模型，整合了资产层和激励层，实现了可复用生态资产的累积，支持基于信用的人机协作经济循环，为AI代理的组织设计提供了可行方案。

Conclusion: AI代理的发展应被重新定义为组织设计问题，关键在于构建基础设施，使可验证的工作留下持久、可复用的产物，并通过价值流动支持持久的人机协作。

Abstract: General-purpose technologies reshape economies less by improving individual tools than by enabling new ways to organize production and coordination. We believe AI agents are approaching a similar inflection point: as foundation models make broad task execution and tool use increasingly accessible, the binding constraint shifts from raw capability to how work is delegated, verified, and rewarded at scale. We introduce EpochX, a credits-native marketplace infrastructure for human-agent production networks. EpochX treats humans and agents as peer participants who can post tasks or claim them. Claimed tasks can be decomposed into subtasks and executed through an explicit delivery workflow with verification and acceptance. Crucially, EpochX is designed so that each completed transaction can produce reusable ecosystem assets, including skills, workflows, execution traces, and distilled experience. These assets are stored with explicit dependency structure, enabling retrieval, composition, and cumulative improvement over time. EpochX also introduces a native credit mechanism to make participation economically viable under real compute costs. Credits lock task bounties, budget delegation, settle rewards upon acceptance, and compensate creators when verified assets are reused. By formalizing the end-to-end transaction model together with its asset and incentive layers, EpochX reframes agentic AI as an organizational design problem: building infrastructures where verifiable work leaves persistent, reusable artifacts, and where value flows support durable human-agent collaboration.

</details>

Conclusion: 当前前沿模型在形式化定理证明方面仍有较大提升空间，FormalProofBench为未来模型改进和评估提供了有价值的基准。

Abstract: We present FormalProofBench, a private benchmark designed to evaluate whether AI models can produce formally verified mathematical proofs at the graduate level. Each task pairs a natural-language problem with a Lean~4 formal statement, and a model must output a Lean proof accepted by the Lean 4 checker. FormalProofBench targets advanced undergraduate and graduate mathematics, with problems drawn from qualifying exams and standard textbooks across topics including analysis, algebra, probability, and logic. We evaluate a range of frontier models with an agentic harness, and find that the best-performing foundation model achieves 33.5% accuracy, with performance dropping rapidly after that. In addition to the accuracy numbers, we also provide empirical analysis of tool-use, failure modes, cost and latency, thereby providing a thorough evaluation of the formal-theorem proving abilities of frontier models.

</details>
