The compute variable is best understood as logical compute, an implementation-agnostic notion of model-side work, while the practical burden of scaling depends on how efficiently real resources are converted into that compute. This abstraction helps explain both why the laws travel so well across settings and why they give rise to a persistent efficiency game in hardware, algorithms, and systems. Once efficiency is made explicit, the main practical question becomes how many efficiency doublings are required to keep scaling productive despite diminishing returns. Under that view, diminishing returns are not only a geometric flattening of the loss curve, but also rising pressure for cost reduction, system-level innovation, and the breakthroughs needed to sustain Moore-like efficiency doublings.

</details>


### [242] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Predicting Hydrogen Sorption in Geological Formations: Thermodynamically Constrained Deep Learning Integrating Classical Adsorption Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28328)
*Mohammad Nooraiepour,Mohammad Masoudi,Zezhang Song,Helge Hellevang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于预测细粒度地质材料中的氢吸附，解决了经典模型在异质样本群体上泛化能力差的问题，实现了高精度预测和跨岩性泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测细粒度地质材料中的氢吸附对于评估地下储氢容量、盖层完整性以及地下能源系统中的氢运移至关重要。然而，经典的等温线模型虽然在单样本层面表现良好，但在跨异质样本群体进行泛化时性能急剧下降（决定系数从单样本拟合的0.80-0.90骤降至聚合多样本数据集的0.09-0.38），存在显著的局限性。

Method: 提出了一个多尺度物理信息神经网络框架。该框架利用包含粘土、页岩和煤炭的1,987条氢吸附等温线测量数据及224条特征吸收测量数据。采用七类物理信息特征工程方案从原始材料表征数据中生成62个具有热力学意义的描述符。在损失函数中通过惩罚权重强制执行饱和极限、单调压力响应和范特霍夫温度依赖性，并采用三阶段基于课程的训练策略。同时使用包含十个成员的架构多样化集成模型进行校准后的不确定性量化。

Result: 优化后的PINN模型在保留测试集上达到了R² = 0.9544，RMSE = 0.0484 mmol/g，MAE = 0.0231 mmol/g的性能指标，实现了98.6%的单调性满足率，且无非物理负预测。在留一岩性验证下，物理信息正则化相比调优后的随机森林模型实现了10-15%的跨岩性泛化优势。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了将经典吸附理论和热力学约束直接嵌入学习过程能够有效解决异质样本泛化问题，热力学约束能够跨越地质边界有意义地迁移，为地下储氢系统的评估提供了可靠的预测工具。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of hydrogen sorption in fine-grained geological materials is essential for evaluating underground hydrogen storage capacity, assessing caprock integrity, and characterizing hydrogen migration in subsurface energy systems. Classical isotherm models perform well at the individual-sample level but fail when generalized across heterogeneous populations, with the coefficient of determination collapsing from 0.80-0.90 for single-sample fits to 0.09-0.38 for aggregated multi-sample datasets. We present a multi-scale physics-informed neural network framework that addresses this limitation by embedding classical adsorption theory and thermodynamic constraints directly into the learning process. The framework utilizes 1,987 hydrogen sorption isotherm measurements across clays, shales, coals, supplemented by 224 characteristic uptake measurements. A seven-category physics-informed feature engineering scheme generates 62 thermodynamically meaningful descriptors from raw material characterization data. The loss function enforces saturation limits, a monotonic pressure response, and Van't Hoff temperature dependence via penalty weighting, while a three-phase curriculum-based training strategy ensures stable integration of competing physical constraints. An architecture-diverse ensemble of ten members provides calibrated uncertainty quantification, with post-hoc temperature scaling achieving target prediction interval coverage. The optimized PINN achieves R2 = 0.9544, RMSE = 0.0484 mmol/g, and MAE = 0.0231 mmol/g on the held-out test set, with 98.6% monotonicity satisfaction and zero non-physical negative predictions. Physics-informed regularization yields a 10-15% cross-lithology generalization advantage over a well-tuned random forest under leave-one-lithology-out validation, confirming that thermodynamic constraints transfer meaningfully across geological boundaries.

</details>


### [243] [Detecting low left ventricular ejection fraction from ECG using an interpretable and scalable predictor-driven framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28532)
*Ya Zhou,Tianxiang Hao,Ziyi Cai,Haojie Zhu,Hejun He,Jia Liu,Xiaohan Fan,Jing Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出ECGPD-LEF框架，通过整合基础模型衍生的诊断概率与可解释性建模，实现了低左心室射血分数的精准筛查，在内外部验证中均优于端到端基线模型，并揭示了心室功能障碍在结构化诊断概率表示中的内在编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低左心室射血分数常在进展为症状性心力衰竭前未被检出，亟需可扩展的筛查策略。现有AI-ECG方法存在明显局限：端到端黑盒模型缺乏可解释性，而依赖商业ECG测量算法的表格系统性能欠佳，无法平衡预测性能与机制透明度。

Method: 提出ECGPD-LEF框架，将基础模型衍生的诊断概率与可解释建模相整合。在EchoNext基准数据集（72,475对ECG-超声心动图配对数据）上训练，并在预定义的独立内部队列（n=5,442）和外部队列（n=16,017）中验证。框架通过结构化诊断概率表示实现LEF风险估计。

Result: 框架在中度LEF检测中表现稳健：内部验证AUROC 88.4%、F1 64.5%；外部验证AUROC 86.8%、F1 53.6%，在各人口统计学和临床亚组中均持续优于基准提供的端到端基线模型。可解释性分析识别出高影响预测因子：正常ECG、不完全左束支传导阻滞、前侧壁导联心内膜下损伤。这些预测因子可独立实现零样本式推理（内部AUROC 75.3-81.0%，外部AUROC 71.6-78.6%），无需任务特异性重训练。

Conclusion: 该框架成功调和了预测性能与机制透明度，证明心室功能障碍内在编码于结构化诊断概率表示中。框架支持通过增加预测因子实现可扩展增强，并可与现有AI-ECG系统无缝整合，为临床可扩展的LEF筛查提供了兼具高性能与可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Low left ventricular ejection fraction (LEF) frequently remains undetected until progression to symptomatic heart failure, underscoring the need for scalable screening strategies. Although artificial intelligence-enabled electrocardiography (AI-ECG) has shown promise, existing approaches rely solely on end-to-end black-box models with limited interpretability or on tabular systems dependent on commercial ECG measurement algorithms with suboptimal performance. We introduced ECG-based Predictor-Driven LEF (ECGPD-LEF), a structured framework that integrates foundation model-derived diagnostic probabilities with interpretable modeling for detecting LEF from ECG. Trained on the benchmark EchoNext dataset comprising 72,475 ECG-echocardiogram pairs and evaluated in predefined independent internal (n=5,442) and external (n=16,017) cohorts, our framework achieved robust discrimination for moderate LEF (internal AUROC 88.4%, F1 64.5%; external AUROC 86.8%, F1 53.6%), consistently outperforming the official end-to-end baseline provided with the benchmark across demographic and clinical subgroups. Interpretability analyses identified high-impact predictors, including normal ECG, incomplete left bundle branch block, and subendocardial injury in anterolateral leads, driving LEF risk estimation. Notably, these predictors independently enabled zero-shot-like inference without task-specific retraining (internal AUROC 75.3-81.0%; external AUROC 71.6-78.6%), indicating that ventricular dysfunction is intrinsically encoded within structured diagnostic probability representations. This framework reconciles predictive performance with mechanistic transparency, supporting scalable enhancement through additional predictors and seamless integration with existing AI-ECG systems.

</details>


### [244] [CirrusBench: Evaluating LLM-based Agents Beyond Correctness in Real-World Cloud Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28569)
*Yi Yu,Guangquan Hu,Chenghuang Shen,Xingyan Liu,Jing Gu,Hangyi Sun,Junzhuo Ma,Weiting Liu,Jianfeng Liu,Mingyue Pu,Yu Wang,Zhengdong Xiao,Rui Xie,Longjiu Luo,Qianrong Wang,Gurong Cui,Honglin Qiao,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了基于真实云服务工单的评估框架CirrusBench，发现当前最先进的大模型在复杂多轮任务中表现不佳，无法满足客服场景的高效性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于合成环境的LLM智能体基准测试无法捕捉真实客户输入的多样性和不可预测性，且忽略了实际部署所必需的解决效率。云服务客户-助手交互具有高技术复杂性和长期依赖性，需要针对真实场景的评估框架。

Method: 构建基于真实云服务工单数据的评估框架CirrusBench，保留技术服务环境中的复杂多轮逻辑链和真实工具依赖；提出以客户为中心的评估指标，包括归一化效率指数（Normalized Efficiency Index）和多轮延迟（Multi-Turn Latency），显式量化解决效率。

Result: 实验表明，尽管最先进模型展现出较强的推理能力，但在复杂、真实的多轮任务中经常遇到困难，无法满足客户服务所需的高效率标准，暴露了LLM智能体在实际技术服务应用中的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: CirrusBench为LLM智能体在实用技术服务应用中的未来发展指明了关键方向，强调了在真实场景中提升解决效率和多轮任务处理能力的重要性。

Abstract: The increasing agentic capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled their deployment in real-world applications, such as cloud services, where customer-assistant interactions exhibit high technical complexity and long-horizon dependencies, making robustness and resolution efficiency critical for customer satisfaction. However, existing benchmarks for LLM-based agents largely rely on synthetic environments that fail to capture the diversity and unpredictability of authentic customer inputs, often ignoring the resolution efficiency essential for real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we introduce CirrusBench, a novel evaluation framework distinguished by its foundation in real-world data from authentic cloud service tickets. CirrusBench preserves the intricate multi-turn logical chains and realistic tool dependencies inherent to technical service environments. Moving beyond execution correctness, we introduce novel Customer-Centric metrics to define agent success, quantifying service quality through metrics such as the Normalized Efficiency Index and Multi-Turn Latency to explicitly measure resolution efficiency. Experiments utilizing our framework reveal that while state-of-the-art models demonstrate strong reasoning capabilities, they frequently struggle in complex, realistic multi-turn tasks and fail to meet the high-efficiency standards required for customer service, highlighting critical directions for the future development of LLM-based agents in practical technical service applications. CirrusBench evaluation framework is released at: https://github.com/CirrusAI

</details>


### [245] [Machine Learning-Assisted High-Dimensional Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28346)
*Wan Tian,Hui Yang,Zhouhui Lian,Lingyue Zhang,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习辅助的高维矩阵估计方法，通过将线性化交替方向乘子法(LADMM)与神经网络结合，提高了估计精度并加速收敛，理论证明了重参数化LADMM具有更快的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高维矩阵估计研究主要关注估计器的理论性质（如一致性和稀疏性），而在很大程度上忽略了高维设置中固有的计算挑战。受学习优化方法的最新进展启发，作者探索利用机器学习来辅助高维矩阵估计问题。

Method: 首先提出基于线性化交替方向乘子法(LADMM)的求解过程；然后引入可学习参数，并用神经网络对迭代方案中的近端算子进行建模，从而提高估计精度和加速收敛；理论证明了LADMM的收敛性，以及重参数化版本的收敛性、收敛速率和单调性。

Result: 理论上证明了重参数化LADMM具有更快的收敛速率；该方法适用于高维协方差矩阵和精度矩阵的估计；通过在不同结构和维度的高维矩阵上与多种经典优化算法进行比较，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的重参数化理论和方法适用于高维协方差矩阵和精度矩阵的估计，实验结果表明该方法在估计精度和收敛速度方面优于传统优化算法，为高维矩阵估计提供了一种有效的学习优化解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient estimation of high-dimensional matrices-including covariance and precision matrices-is a cornerstone of modern multivariate statistics. Most existing studies have focused primarily on the theoretical properties of the estimators (e.g., consistency and sparsity), while largely overlooking the computational challenges inherent in high-dimensional settings. Motivated by recent advances in learning-based optimization method-which integrate data-driven structures with classical optimization algorithms-we explore high-dimensional matrix estimation assisted by machine learning. Specifically, for the optimization problem of high-dimensional matrix estimation, we first present a solution procedure based on the Linearized Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (LADMM). We then introduce learnable parameters and model the proximal operators in the iterative scheme with neural networks, thereby improving estimation accuracy and accelerating convergence. Theoretically, we first prove the convergence of LADMM, and then establish the convergence, convergence rate, and monotonicity of its reparameterized counterpart; importantly, we show that the reparameterized LADMM enjoys a faster convergence rate. Notably, the proposed reparameterization theory and methodology are applicable to the estimation of both high-dimensional covariance and precision matrices. We validate the effectiveness of our method by comparing it with several classical optimization algorithms across different structures and dimensions of high-dimensional matrices.

</details>


### [246] [ChemCLIP: Bridging Organic and Inorganic Anticancer Compounds Through Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28575)
*Mohamad Koohi-Moghadam,Hongzhe Sun,Hongyan Li,Kyongtae Tyler Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ChemCLIP框架，利用对比学习跨越有机小分子与金属配合物的界限，学习基于共同抗癌活性的统一表示，并系统评估了四种分子编码策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗癌药物发现传统上将有机小分子和金属配合物视为分离的化学领域，尽管它们具有共同的生物学目标，但知识转移受限。现有数据差异显著，有机化合物拥有海量筛选数据库，而表征的金属配合物仅数千种。

Method: 构建了包含44,854个有机化合物和5,164个金属配合物的互补数据集，涵盖60种癌细胞系。提出双编码器对比学习框架ChemCLIP，通过基于活性的困难负样本挖掘，将结构不同的化合物映射到共享的256维嵌入空间。系统评估了Morgan指纹、ChemBERTa、MolFormer和Chemprop四种分子编码策略。

Result: Morgan指纹表现最优，平均对齐比率为0.899，下游分类AUC分别为0.859（无机物）和0.817（有机物）。在共享嵌入空间中，生物相似的化合物无论化学类别如何都会聚类在一起。

Conclusion: 对比学习是统一不同化学领域的有效策略，为多模态化学应用中的编码器选择提供了实证指导。该工作的影响超越抗癌药物发现，适用于任何需要跨域化学知识转移的场景。

Abstract: The discovery of anticancer therapeutics has traditionally treated organic small molecules and metal-based coordination complexes as separate chemical domains, limiting knowledge transfer despite their shared biological objectives. This disparity is particularly pronounced in available data, with extensive screening databases for organic compounds compared to only a few thousand characterized metal complexes. Here, we introduce ChemCLIP, a dual-encoder contrastive learning framework that bridges this organic-inorganic divide by learning unified representations based on shared anticancer activities rather than structural similarity. We compiled complementary datasets comprising 44,854 unique organic compounds and 5,164 unique metal complexes, standardized across 60 cancer cell lines. By training parallel encoders with activity-aware hard negative mining, we mapped structurally distinct compounds into a shared 256-dimensional embedding space where biologically similar compounds cluster together regardless of chemical class. We systematically evaluated four molecular encoding strategies: Morgan fingerprints, ChemBERTa, MolFormer, and Chemprop, through quantitative alignment metrics, embedding visualizations, and downstream classification tasks. Morgan fingerprints achieved superior performance with an average alignment ratio of 0.899 and downstream classification AUCs of 0.859 (inorganic) and 0.817 (organic). This work establishes contrastive learning as an effective strategy for unifying disparate chemical domains and provides empirical guidance for encoder selection in multi-modal chemistry applications, with implications extending beyond anticancer drug discovery to any scenario requiring cross-domain chemical knowledge transfer.

</details>


### [247] [Label-efficient Training Updates for Malware Detection over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28396)
*Luca Minnei,Cristian Manca,Giorgio Piras,Angelo Sotgiu,Maura Pintor,Daniele Ghiani,Davide Maiorca,Giorgio Giacinto,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模型无关的框架，评估了主动学习和半监督学习技术（单独或组合使用）在Android和Windows恶意软件检测中的效果，结合使用可减少90%的人工标注成本，同时保持与全标注重训练相当的检测性能，并引入了特征级漂移分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于机器学习的恶意软件检测器在面对现实环境中合法软件和恶意软件不断演变导致的分布漂移时，性能会随时间下降。虽然定期重训练可以解决此问题，但人工标注成本高昂。现有研究存在两个主要问题：（i）与特定检测器架构紧密耦合且局限于特定恶意软件领域，导致比较不统一；（ii）缺乏分析分布漂移的一致方法论。

Method: 作者提出了一个模型无关的框架，评估了广泛的主动学习（AL）和半监督学习（SSL）技术，包括单独使用和组合使用，应用于Android和Windows恶意软件检测。此外，引入了一种特征级漂移分析方法，用于测量特征随时间的稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，将AL和SSL技术结合使用，在Android和Windows两个领域中均可减少高达90%的人工标注成本，同时达到与全标注重训练相当的检测性能。特征级漂移分析显示特征的稳定性与检测器性能存在相关性。

Conclusion: 该研究详细揭示了AL和SSL在分布漂移下的行为表现以及如何成功组合使用，为设计长期有效的恶意软件检测器提供了实用见解和指导。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML)-based detectors are becoming essential to counter the proliferation of malware. However, common ML algorithms are not designed to cope with the dynamic nature of real-world settings, where both legitimate and malicious software evolve. This distribution drift causes models trained under static assumptions to degrade over time unless they are continuously updated. Regularly retraining these models, however, is expensive, since labeling new acquired data requires costly manual analysis by security experts. To reduce labeling costs and address distribution drift in malware detection, prior work explored active learning (AL) and semi-supervised learning (SSL) techniques. Yet, existing studies (i) are tightly coupled to specific detector architectures and restricted to a specific malware domain, resulting in non-uniform comparisons; and (ii) lack a consistent methodology for analyzing the distribution drift, despite the critical sensitivity of the malware domain to temporal changes. In this work, we bridge this gap by proposing a model-agnostic framework that evaluates an extensive set of AL and SSL techniques, isolated and combined, for Android and Windows malware detection. We show that these techniques, when combined, can reduce manual annotation costs by up to 90% across both domains while achieving comparable detection performance to full-labeling retraining. We also introduce a methodology for feature-level drift analysis that measures feature stability over time, showing its correlation with the detector performance. Overall, our study provides a detailed understanding of how AL and SSL behave under distribution drift and how they can be successfully combined, offering practical insights for the design of effective detectors over time.

</details>


### [248] [Mixture-Model Preference Learning for Many-Objective Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28410)
*Manisha Dubey,Sebastiaan De Peuter,Wanrong Wang,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于贝叶斯框架的偏好多目标优化方法，通过学习潜在偏好原型并设计混合查询策略，有效解决了多目标优化中权衡空间扩张和异构价值结构的问题，并在理论和实验上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好驱动的多目标优化面临两大核心障碍：一是权衡空间随目标数量增加而急剧扩张，二是人类价值结构具有异构性和情境依赖性；传统方法通常假设单一固定的效用函数，难以有效应对上述挑战。

Method: 提出一种贝叶斯框架，将潜在偏好原型作为狄利克雷过程混合模型的成分进行学习，对原型及其权重的不确定性进行建模；设计了针对模式识别和模式内权衡的混合查询策略，以高效获取偏好信息；基于此提出了混合感知的贝叶斯优化过程，并在温和假设下给出了简单遗憾界保证。

Result: 在合成和真实的多目标基准测试中，该方法在性能上优于标准基线；混合感知诊断揭示了传统遗憾指标无法捕捉的结构信息，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯框架和混合查询策略有效解决了偏好多目标优化中的异构性和权衡空间扩张问题，理论遗憾保证和实验结果均表明该方法具有显著优势，为复杂多目标优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Preference-based many-objective optimization faces two obstacles: an expanding space of trade-offs and heterogeneous, context-dependent human value structures. Towards this, we propose a Bayesian framework that learns a small set of latent preference archetypes rather than assuming a single fixed utility function, modelling them as components of a Dirichlet-process mixture with uncertainty over both archetypes and their weights. To query efficiently, we designing hybrid queries that target information about (i) mode identity and (ii) within-mode trade-offs. Under mild assumptions, we provide a simple regret guarantee for the resulting mixture-aware Bayesian optimization procedure. Empirically, our method outperforms standard baselines on synthetic and real-world many-objective benchmarks, and mixture-aware diagnostics reveal structure that regret alone fails to capture.

</details>


### [249] [Information-Theoretic Limits of Safety Verification for Self-Improving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28650)
*Arsenios Scrivens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于分类器的安全门在幂律风险调度下无法同时实现有界累积风险与无限效益（不可能性定理），但基于验证器的方法可以突破该限制，在GPT-2上实现了条件风险为零且TPR=0.352。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究安全门是否能在保持累积风险有界的前提下，允许无界的有益自我修改。作者通过形式化对偶条件（风险有界与效用无界）来探索这一根本性问题。

Method: 1. 使用Hölder不等式和NP计数方法证明分类器的不可能性；2. 推导有限视界下的效用上界公式；3. 提出使用Lipschitz球验证器突破分类器限制；4. 为预LayerNorm变换器在LoRA下建立形式化Lipschitz界。

Result: 定理1和4证明：幂律风险调度(p>1)下分类器效用必有界；定理5给出精确效用上界U*(N,B)=N*TPR_NP(B/N)，在N=10^6时仅~87；定理2证明验证器可实现delta=0且TPR>0；GPT-2实验：d_LoRA=147,456条件下delta=0，TPR=0.352。

Conclusion: 分类器方法存在根本理论局限，而验证器方法提供了严格的性能分离。对于LLM规模的安全门设计，应采用验证器而非分类器架构，后者在大规模场景下严重受限（效用仅为验证器的约0.02%）。

Abstract: Can a safety gate permit unbounded beneficial self-modification while maintaining bounded cumulative risk? We formalize this question through dual conditions -- requiring sum delta_n < infinity (bounded risk) and sum TPR_n = infinity (unbounded utility) -- and establish a theory of their (in)compatibility.
  Classification impossibility (Theorem 1): For power-law risk schedules delta_n = O(n^{-p}) with p > 1, any classifier-based gate under overlapping safe/unsafe distributions satisfies TPR_n <= C_alpha * delta_n^beta via Holder's inequality, forcing sum TPR_n < infinity. This impossibility is exponent-optimal (Theorem 3). A second independent proof via the NP counting method (Theorem 4) yields a 13% tighter bound without Holder's inequality.
  Universal finite-horizon ceiling (Theorem 5): For any summable risk schedule, the exact maximum achievable classifier utility is U*(N, B) = N * TPR_NP(B/N), growing as exp(O(sqrt(log N))) -- subpolynomial. At N = 10^6 with budget B = 1.0, a classifier extracts at most U* ~ 87 versus a verifier's ~500,000.
  Verification escape (Theorem 2): A Lipschitz ball verifier achieves delta = 0 with TPR > 0, escaping the impossibility. Formal Lipschitz bounds for pre-LayerNorm transformers under LoRA enable LLM-scale verification. The separation is strict. We validate on GPT-2 (d_LoRA = 147,456): conditional delta = 0 with TPR = 0.352. Comprehensive empirical validation is in the companion paper [D2].

</details>


### [250] [AMIGO: Agentic Multi-Image Grounding Oracle Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28662)
*Min Wang,Ata Mahjoubfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AMIGO基准测试，用于评估智能体视觉语言模型在多图像、长时域交互中的隐式目标识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉语言模型评估主要关注单图像、单轮交互的正确性，但实际应用中模型需要进行扩展交互。缺乏针对长时域、多图像场景下的评估基准，特别是在不确定性下进行问题选择、跨轮次约束跟踪和细粒度判别方面的能力评估。

Method: 设计AMIGO基准测试，设置Oracle私选目标图像，模型需通过询问属性相关的Yes/No/Unsure问题序列来识别目标，采用严格协议惩罚无效动作为Skip。支持可控的Oracle不完美性以探测不一致反馈下的鲁棒性和验证行为。实例化为"猜我偏好的裙子"任务。

Result: 报告涵盖结果和交互质量的多个指标，包括识别成功率、证据验证、效率、协议合规性、噪声容忍度和轨迹级别诊断，全面评估模型在长时域交互中的表现。

Conclusion: AMIGO为评估智能体视觉语言模型在复杂多图像场景下的长时域能力提供了系统性基准，揭示了现有模型在问题选择、约束跟踪和细粒度判别方面的挑战。

Abstract: Agentic vision-language models increasingly act through extended interactions, but most evaluations still focus on single-image, single-turn correctness. We introduce AMIGO (Agentic Multi-Image Grounding Oracle Benchmark), a long-horizon benchmark for hidden-target identification over galleries of visually similar images. In AMIGO, the oracle privately selects a target image, and the model must recover it by asking a sequence of attribute-focused Yes/No/Unsure questions under a strict protocol that penalizes invalid actions with Skip. This setting stresses (i) question selection under uncertainty, (ii) consistent constraint tracking across turns, and (iii) fine-grained discrimination as evidence accumulates. AMIGO also supports controlled oracle imperfections to probe robustness and verification behavior under inconsistent feedback. We instantiate AMIGO with Guess My Preferred Dress task and report metrics covering both outcomes and interaction quality, including identification success, evidence verification, efficiency, protocol compliance, noise tolerance, and trajectory-level diagnostics.

</details>


### [251] [Stepwise Credit Assignment for GRPO on Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28718)
*Yash Savani,Branislav Kveton,Yuchen Liu,Yilin Wang,Jing Shi,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Nikos Vlassis,Krishna Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stepwise-Flow-GRPO方法，通过基于每步奖励改进的信用分配和DDIM启发的SDE，解决了Flow-GRPO中均匀信用分配忽略扩散生成时间结构的问题，实现了更优的样本效率和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow-GRPO将强化学习应用于流模型时采用均匀信用分配，忽略了扩散生成的时间结构：早期步骤决定构图和内容（低频结构），后期步骤解决细节和纹理（高频细节）。此外，仅基于最终图像分配均匀信用可能会错误地奖励次优的中间步骤，特别是当错误在扩散轨迹后期被纠正时。

Method: 提出Stepwise-Flow-GRPO方法，基于每步的奖励改进进行信用分配。利用Tweedie公式获得中间奖励估计，引入基于增益的优势函数。同时引入DDIM启发的SDE，在保持策略梯度随机性的同时提高奖励质量。

Result: 该方法实现了更优的样本效率和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 通过考虑扩散生成的时间结构并实施逐步信用分配，Stepwise-Flow-GRPO有效改进了流模型的强化学习应用，为生成模型的训练提供了更高效的策略优化方法。

Abstract: Flow-GRPO successfully applies reinforcement learning to flow models, but uses uniform credit assignment across all steps. This ignores the temporal structure of diffusion generation: early steps determine composition and content (low-frequency structure), while late steps resolve details and textures (high-frequency details). Moreover, assigning uniform credit based solely on the final image can inadvertently reward suboptimal intermediate steps, especially when errors are corrected later in the diffusion trajectory. We propose Stepwise-Flow-GRPO, which assigns credit based on each step's reward improvement. By leveraging Tweedie's formula to obtain intermediate reward estimates and introducing gain-based advantages, our method achieves superior sample efficiency and faster convergence. We also introduce a DDIM-inspired SDE that improves reward quality while preserving stochasticity for policy gradients.

</details>


### [252] [Geometry-aware similarity metrics for neural representations on Riemannian and statistical manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28764)
*N Alex Cayco Gajic,Arthur Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了度量相似性分析（MSA）方法，利用黎曼几何工具比较神经表征的内在几何特性，解决了现有方法仅比较外在几何而无法捕捉神经网络本质差异的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的相似性度量方法主要比较神经表征在状态空间中的外在几何特性，而非其内在几何特性，因此可能无法捕捉到本质上不同的神经网络解决方案之间微妙但关键的差异。在流形假设下，需要一种能够比较神经表征内在几何特性的新方法。

Method: 提出度量相似性分析（MSA），一种利用黎曼几何工具在流形假设下比较神经表征内在几何特性的新方法。该方法能够比较神经网络的内在几何特性而非外在几何特性。

Result: MSA方法可以：i) 在具有不同学习机制的深度网络中解耦神经计算特征；ii) 比较非线性动力学；iii) 研究扩散模型。证明了该方法在多种场景下的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: MSA提供了一个数学上严谨且广泛适用的框架，通过比较神经计算的内在几何特性来理解神经计算背后的机制，为神经网络表征分析开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Similarity measures are widely used to interpret the representational geometries used by neural networks to solve tasks. Yet, because existing methods compare the extrinsic geometry of representations in state space, rather than their intrinsic geometry, they may fail to capture subtle yet crucial distinctions between fundamentally different neural network solutions. Here, we introduce metric similarity analysis (MSA), a novel method which leverages tools from Riemannian geometry to compare the intrinsic geometry of neural representations under the manifold hypothesis. We show that MSA can be used to i) disentangle features of neural computations in deep networks with different learning regimes, ii) compare nonlinear dynamics, and iii) investigate diffusion models. Hence, we introduce a mathematically grounded and broadly applicable framework to understand the mechanisms behind neural computations by comparing their intrinsic geometries.

</details>


### [253] [IsoQuant: Hardware-Aligned SO(4) Isoclinic Rotations for LLM KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28430)
*Zhongping Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IsoQuant提出基于四元数代数和SO(4)等倾分解的分块旋转框架，在保持重建精度的同时，相比RotorQuant实现约4.5×-4.7×的内核级加速，显著降低了正交特征去相关的存储和计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交特征去相关对低比特在线向量量化有效，但稠密随机正交变换会带来高昂的O(d²)存储和计算成本。现有方法RotorQuant虽通过分块3D Clifford转子降低了成本，但其3D分区与现代硬件对齐性差，且局部混合能力有限。

Method: 提出IsoQuant框架，基于四元数代数和SO(4)的等倾分解。将每个4D块表示为四元数，应用闭式变换T(v)=q_L v q_R̄。包含两个主要变体：IsoQuant-Full实现完整SO(4)旋转，IsoQuant-Fast仅保留一个等倾因子以降低成本；框架还支持轻量级2D特例。

Result: 在d=128时，IsoQuant-Full将前向旋转成本从RotorQuant的约2,408 FMAs降至1,024，IsoQuant-Fast进一步降至512。在18个融合CUDA设置（d∈{128,256,512}，位宽{2,3,4}，FP16/FP32执行）中，IsoQuant相比RotorQuant实现约4.5×-4.7×的平均内核级加速，峰值加速超过6×，同时保持相当的重建MSE。

Conclusion: IsoQuant通过四元数代数和等倾分解实现了高效的正交特征去相关，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持了量化精度。当前验证仅限于合成归一化向量上的stage-1量化-反量化路径，端到端KV缓存评估留作未来工作。

Abstract: Orthogonal feature decorrelation is effective for low-bit online vector quantization, but dense random orthogonal transforms incur prohibitive $O(d^2)$ storage and compute. RotorQuant reduces this cost with blockwise $3$D Clifford rotors, yet the resulting $3$D partition is poorly aligned with modern hardware and offers limited local mixing.
  We propose \textbf{IsoQuant}, a blockwise rotation framework based on quaternion algebra and the isoclinic decomposition of $SO(4)$. It represents each $4$D block as a quaternion and applies a closed-form transform $T(v)=q_L v \overline{q_R}$. This yields two main variants: \emph{IsoQuant-Full}, which realizes the full $SO(4)$ rotation, and \emph{IsoQuant-Fast}, which keeps only one isoclinic factor for lower cost; the framework also admits a lightweight $2$D special case. At $d=128$, IsoQuant-Full reduces forward rotation cost from about $2{,}408$ FMAs in RotorQuant to $1{,}024$, while IsoQuant-Fast further reduces it to $512$. Across $18$ fused CUDA settings with $d \in {128,256,512}$, bit widths ${2,3,4}$, and FP16/FP32 execution, IsoQuant achieves mean kernel-level speedups of about $4.5\times$--$4.7\times$ over RotorQuant while maintaining comparable reconstruction MSE, with peak speedups above $6\times$. Current validation is limited to the stage-1 quantize--dequantize path on synthetic normalized vectors; end-to-end KV-cache evaluation remains future work.

</details>


### [254] [Unrestrained Simplex Denoising for Discrete Data. A Non-Markovian Approach Applied to Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28572)
*Yoann Boget,Alexandros Kalousis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Simplex Denoising，一种在概率单纯形上运行的生成框架，通过非马尔可夫噪声方案移除了不必要的约束，在离散结构生成任务上超越了现有的离散扩散和流匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去噪模型（如扩散模型或流匹配）在处理离散结构时存在局限性：大多数方法直接在离散状态空间中操作，导致状态变化突兀。因此需要一种新的框架来改进离散结构的生成建模。

Method: 引入了一种简单的生成框架——Simplex Denoising，它运行在概率单纯形上。核心创新是非马尔可夫噪声方案：对于给定的干净数据点，不同时间的噪声表示是条件独立的。这种方法在保留去噪生成模型理论保证的同时，移除了不必要的约束，从而简化了公式化表达。

Result: 在合成和真实世界的图基准测试中，无约束单纯形去噪方法超越了强有力的离散扩散和流匹配基线模型。

Conclusion: 概率单纯形是离散生成建模的有效框架，为该领域提供了一个简单而有效的新方向。

Abstract: Denoising models such as Diffusion or Flow Matching have recently advanced generative modeling for discrete structures, yet most approaches either operate directly in the discrete state space, causing abrupt state changes. We introduce simplex denoising, a simple yet effective generative framework that operates on the probability simplex. The key idea is a non-Markovian noising scheme in which, for a given clean data point, noisy representations at different times are conditionally independent. While preserving the theoretical guarantees of denoising-based generative models, our method removes unnecessary constraints, thereby improving performance and simplifying the formulation. Empirically, \emph{unrestrained simplex denoising} surpasses strong discrete diffusion and flow-matching baselines across synthetic and real-world graph benchmarks. These results highlight the probability simplex as an effective framework for discrete generative modeling.

</details>


### [255] [Physics-Informed Framework for Impact Identification in Aerospace Composites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28593)
*Natália Ribeiro Marinho,Richard Loendersloot,Jan Willem Wiegman,Frank Grooteman,Tiedo Tinga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个物理信息告知的冲击识别框架（Phy-ID），通过融合物理知识与数据驱动推断，实现了在测量条件退化情况下的可靠、物理一致且数据高效的冲击识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的冲击识别方法在测量数据不足或噪声干扰严重时，往往难以保证推断结果的物理一致性和数值稳定性。为了解决这一问题，作者旨在开发一种能够系统融合物理知识与数据驱动方法的统一建模策略，以限制非物理解并增强模型在恶劣条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了Phy-ID框架，通过整合观测、归纳和学习偏置来融合物理知识。具体措施包括：利用基于物理的能量指标构建输入空间；通过架构设计约束可行解；通过混合损失函数强制执行控制关系。采用解耦推断公式作为用例，使用分离的代理模型推断冲击速度和冲击体质量，并通过强制动能一致性计算冲击能量。

Result: 实验评估表明，推断的冲击速度和冲击体质量的平均绝对百分比误差低于8%，冲击能量的误差低于10%。在数据可用性降低和测量噪声增加的情况下性能稳定；当训练数据包含损伤响应时，该方法在原始和损伤状态下的分布外案例中表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 系统性地集成物理信息偏置能够实现可靠、物理一致且数据高效的冲击识别，验证了该方法在实际监测系统中具有重要的应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel physics-informed impact identification (Phy-ID) framework. The proposed method integrates observational, inductive, and learning biases to combine physical knowledge with data-driven inference in a unified modelling strategy, achieving physically consistent and numerically stable impact identification. The physics-informed approach structures the input space using physics-based energy indicators, constrains admissible solutions via architectural design, and enforces governing relations via hybrid loss formulations. Together, these mechanisms limit non-physical solutions and stabilise inference under degraded measurement conditions. A disjoint inference formulation is used as a representative use case to demonstrate the framework capabilities, in which impact velocity and impactor mass are inferred through decoupled surrogate models, and impact energy is computed by enforcing kinetic energy consistency. Experimental evaluations show mean absolute percentage errors below 8% for inferred impact velocity and impactor mass and below 10% for impact energy. Additional analyses confirm stable performance under reduced data availability and increased measurement noise, as well as generalisation for out-of-distribution cases across pristine and damaged regimes when damaged responses are included in training. These results indicate that the systematic integration of physics-informed biases enables reliable, physically consistent, and data-efficient impact identification, highlighting the potential of the approach for practical monitoring systems.

</details>


### [256] [Optimistic Actor-Critic with Parametric Policies for Linear Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28595)
*Max Qiushi Lin,Reza Asad,Kevin Tan,Haque Ishfaq,Csaba Szepesvari,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性MDP提出了一种乐观Actor-Critic框架，通过参数化对数线性策略和Langevin蒙特卡洛采样，在保持最先进样本复杂度的同时，使算法更贴近实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Actor-Critic方法的理论分析存在局限性：要么通过强假设回避探索问题，要么分析不切实际且算法复杂的方法。此外，针对线性MDP的方法通常采用自然策略梯度并构建隐式策略，这些策略采样成本高昂，导致环境交互效率低下。

Method: 聚焦有限视界线性MDP，提出乐观Actor-Critic框架，使用参数化对数线性策略。具体地，为Actor引入可处理的logit-matching回归目标；为Critic使用基于Langevin蒙特卡洛的近似Thompson采样获得乐观价值估计。

Result: 所提算法在策略和离策略设置下分别达到Õ(ε^{-4})和Õ(ε^{-2})的样本复杂度，与先前理论工作相比达到了最先进水平，同时算法更符合实际应用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功填补了理论分析与实际应用之间的差距，在保持理论样本复杂度最优性的前提下，提出了更具实用性的Actor-Critic算法框架。

Abstract: Although actor-critic methods have been successful in practice, their theoretical analyses have several limitations. Specifically, existing theoretical work either sidesteps the exploration problem by making strong assumptions or analyzes impractical methods with complicated algorithmic modifications. Moreover, the actor-critic methods analyzed for linear MDPs often employ natural policy gradient (NPG) and construct "implicit" policies without explicit parameterization. Such policies are computationally expensive to sample from, making the environment interactions inefficient. To that end, we focus on the finite-horizon linear MDPs and propose an optimistic actor-critic framework that uses parametric log-linear policies. In particular, we introduce a tractable \textit{logit-matching} regression objective for the actor. For the critic, we use approximate Thompson sampling via Langevin Monte Carlo to obtain optimistic value estimates. We prove that the resulting algorithm achieves $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-4})$ and $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity in the on-policy and off-policy setting, respectively. Our results match prior theoretical works in achieving the state-of-the-art sample complexity, while our algorithm is more aligned with practice.

</details>


### [257] [Position: Explainable AI is Causality in Disguise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28597)
*Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出XAI领域的持续分歧并非源于缺乏基本真值，而是因为基本真值是控制相关系统的因果模型。作者将XAI问题重新定义为因果查询，证明了因果模型对XAI的必要性和充分性，呼吁社区围绕因果发现来摆脱当前的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前XAI领域方法碎片化严重，存在指标冲突、健全性检查失败、鲁棒性和公平性争议等问题。普遍观点将问题归因于缺乏定义"正确解释"的基本真值，导致领域缺乏共识。

Method: 提出将XAI关于数据、模型或决策的查询重新定义为因果查询，从因果推理角度分析XAI问题，证明因果模型对可解释性的必要性和充分性。

Result: 证明了XAI持续分歧的根本原因不是缺乏基本真值，而是基本真值以难以捉摸的因果模型形式存在；确立了因果模型作为XAI理论基础的地位。

Conclusion: 没有因果基础的XAI将失去根基，社区应聚焦于高级概念和因果发现研究，以突破当前的困境并建立统一的理论框架。

Abstract: The demand for Explainable AI (XAI) has triggered an explosion of methods, producing a landscape so fragmented that we now rely on surveys of surveys. Yet, fundamental challenges persist: conflicting metrics, failed sanity checks, and unresolved debates over robustness and fairness. The only consensus on how to achieve explainability is a lack of one. This has led many to point to the absence of a ground truth for defining ``the'' correct explanation as the main culprit.
  This position paper posits that the persistent discord in XAI arises not from an absent ground truth but from a ground truth that exists, albeit as an elusive and challenging target: the causal model that governs the relevant system. By reframing XAI queries about data, models, or decisions as causal inquiries, we prove the necessity and sufficiency of causal models for XAI. We contend that without this causal grounding, XAI remains unmoored. Ultimately, we encourage the community to converge around advanced concept and causal discovery to escape this entrenched uncertainty.

</details>


### [258] [LACE: Loss-Adaptive Capacity Expansion for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28611)
*Shivnath Tathe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LACE方法，通过监控损失信号自适应扩展模型表征容量，在持续学习中实现按需扩容，避免预定义模型宽度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中的固定表征容量是一个根本性约束：实践者必须在训练前猜测合适的模型宽度，而无法预知数据包含多少不同概念，这导致了资源分配的不确定性。

Method: LACE是一种在线机制，通过监控自身的损失信号来扩展模型的表征容量。当持续损失偏差超过阈值（表明当前容量对新数据不足）时，LACE向投影层添加新维度，并与现有参数联合训练。该方法不需要标签、重放缓冲区或外部控制器。

Result: 在合成和真实数据实验中，LACE仅在域边界触发扩展（100%边界精度，零假阳性），从大固定容量模型的一小部分维度开始即可匹配其准确率，且生成的适配器维度对性能至关重要（移除所有适配器时准确率下降3%）。此外，通过GPT-2激活的层级聚类展示了无监督域分离，发现跨层的U形可分性曲线。

Conclusion: LACE为资源受限环境下的设备端持续学习提供了一种简单有效的解决方案，能够自适应地分配容量，无需外部监督或控制机制，实现了高效的模型容量管理。

Abstract: Fixed representational capacity is a fundamental constraint in continual learning: practitioners must guess an appropriate model width before training, without knowing how many distinct concepts the data contains. We propose LACE (Loss-Adaptive Capacity Expansion), a simple online mechanism that expands a model's representational capacity during training by monitoring its own loss signal. When sustained loss deviation exceeds a threshold - indicating that the current capacity is insufficient for newly encountered data - LACE adds new dimensions to the projection layer and trains them jointly with existing parameters. Across synthetic and real-data experiments, LACE triggers expansions exclusively at domain boundaries (100% boundary precision, zero false positives), matches the accuracy of a large fixed-capacity model while starting from a fraction of its dimensions, and produces adapter dimensions that are collectively critical to performance (3% accuracy drop when all adapters removed). We further demonstrate unsupervised domain separation in GPT-2 activations via layer-wise clustering, showing a U-shaped separability curve across layers that motivates adaptive capacity allocation in deep networks. LACE requires no labels, no replay buffers, and no external controllers, making it suitable for on-device continual learning under resource constraints.

</details>


### [259] [Subspace Optimization for Backpropagation-Free Continual Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28678)
*Damian Sójka,Sebastian Cygert,Marc Masana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一种无需反向传播的持续测试时适应系统，通过在低维子空间中优化归一化层的仿射参数，在多个基准测试上实现了最先进的准确度，同时相比现有方法减少了超过50%的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无导数方法难以平衡运行时效率与学习能力，因为它们要么将更新限制在输入提示上，要么需要持续进行资源密集型的适应而忽略域的稳定性。这种局限性促使作者开发一种既能高效运行又能保持强大适应能力的新方法。

Method: PACE结合协方差矩阵适应进化策略（CMA-ES）与Fastfood投影，在低维子空间中优化高维仿射参数；同时引入适应停止准则和域专用向量库来消除冗余计算，从而提升运行时效率。

Result: 该方法在多个持续分布偏移基准测试上达到了最先进的准确度，相比现有无需反向传播的方法，运行时间减少了超过50%。

Conclusion: PACE成功地解决了无导数持续测试时适应中效率与能力的权衡问题，证明了在低维子空间中优化高维参数并结合适当的计算优化策略，可以实现既高效又准确的模型适应。

Abstract: We introduce PACE, a backpropagation-free continual test-time adaptation system that directly optimizes the affine parameters of normalization layers. Existing derivative-free approaches struggle to balance runtime efficiency with learning capacity, as they either restrict updates to input prompts or require continuous, resource-intensive adaptation regardless of domain stability. To address these limitations, PACE leverages the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy with the Fastfood projection to optimize high-dimensional affine parameters within a low-dimensional subspace, leading to superior adaptive performance. Furthermore, we enhance the runtime efficiency by incorporating an adaptation stopping criterion and a domain-specialized vector bank to eliminate redundant computation. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across multiple benchmarks under continual distribution shifts, reducing runtime by over 50% compared to existing backpropagation-free methods.

</details>


### [260] [See it to Place it: Evolving Macro Placements with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28733)
*Ikechukwu Uchendu,Swati Goel,Karly Hou,Ebrahim Songhori,Kuang-Huei Lee,Joe Wenjie Jiang,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Vincent Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了VeoPlace框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)指导芯片布局规划，在10个基准测试中的9个上超越了先前最佳学习方法，线长减少峰值超过32%，并证明了对分析布局器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 芯片宏单元布局是一个复杂的优化任务，人类设计师严重依赖空间推理能力来安排芯片画布上的组件。假设具有强大视觉推理能力的视觉语言模型可以有效补充现有的基于学习的方法。

Method: 提出VeoPlace（视觉进化优化布局）框架，使用未经微调的VLM通过将基础布局器的动作约束到芯片画布的子区域来指导其行为。通过进化搜索策略迭代优化VLM的建议，以优化最终的布局质量。

Result: 在开源基准测试中，VeoPlace在10个基准中的9个上超越了先前最佳的基于学习方法，线长减少峰值超过32%。此外，证明了该方法对分析布局器具有泛化能力，在所有8个评估基准上均提升了DREAMPlace的性能，提升幅度最高达4.3%。

Conclusion: 该方法为利用基础模型解决复杂物理设计问题的电子设计自动化工具开辟了新的可能性，展示了VLM在芯片设计领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: We propose using Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for macro placement in chip floorplanning, a complex optimization task that has recently shown promising advancements through machine learning methods. Because human designers rely heavily on spatial reasoning to arrange components on the chip canvas, we hypothesize that VLMs with strong visual reasoning abilities can effectively complement existing learning-based approaches. We introduce VeoPlace (Visual Evolutionary Optimization Placement), a novel framework that uses a VLM, without any fine-tuning, to guide the actions of a base placer by constraining them to subregions of the chip canvas. The VLM proposals are iteratively optimized through an evolutionary search strategy with respect to resulting placement quality. On open-source benchmarks, VeoPlace outperforms the best prior learning-based approach on 9 of 10 benchmarks with peak wirelength reductions exceeding 32%. We further demonstrate that VeoPlace generalizes to analytical placers, improving DREAMPlace performance on all 8 evaluated benchmarks with gains up to 4.3%. Our approach opens new possibilities for electronic design automation tools that leverage foundation models to solve complex physical design problems.

</details>


### [261] [Expectation Error Bounds for Transfer Learning in Linear Regression and Linear Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28739)
*Meitong Liu,Christopher Jung,Rui Li,Xue Feng,Han Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在最小二乘回归和欠参数化线性神经网络两种设置下，推导了辅助任务改善主任务泛化误差的充要条件或充分条件，并给出了最优任务权重的解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习中辅助数据何时以及如何帮助主任务的理论理解尚不完善，缺乏精确的理论刻画。

Method: 针对线性回归，推导泛化误差期望的闭式解并进行偏置-方差分解；针对宽度q≤K的欠参数化线性神经网络，证明新的列方向低秩扰动界，推导非渐近泛化误差上界。

Result: 在线性回归中得到辅助任务改善泛化的充要条件及最优任务权重的解析解；在线性神经网络中给出首个非空充分条件，并提供任务权重选择的理论指导。

Conclusion: 本文为两种典型线性设置下的迁移学习提供了精确的理论保证，所提出的矩阵扰动界可独立用于其他问题，合成数据实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: In transfer learning, the learner leverages auxiliary data to improve generalization on a main task. However, the precise theoretical understanding of when and how auxiliary data help remains incomplete. We provide new insights on this issue in two canonical linear settings: ordinary least squares regression and under-parameterized linear neural networks. For linear regression, we derive exact closed-form expressions for the expected generalization error with bias-variance decomposition, yielding necessary and sufficient conditions for auxiliary tasks to improve generalization on the main task. We also derive globally optimal task weights as outputs of solvable optimization programs, with consistency guarantees for empirical estimates. For linear neural networks with shared representations of width $q \leq K$, where $K$ is the number of auxiliary tasks, we derive a non-asymptotic expectation bound on the generalization error, yielding the first non-vacuous sufficient condition for beneficial auxiliary learning in this setting, as well as principled directions for task weight curation. We achieve this by proving a new column-wise low-rank perturbation bound for random matrices, which improves upon existing bounds by preserving fine-grained column structures. Our results are verified on synthetic data simulated with controlled parameters.

</details>


### [262] [Rethinking Language Model Scaling under Transferable Hypersphere Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28743)
*Liliang Ren,Yang Liu,Yelong Shen,Weizhu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperP框架，首次实现超球面优化下的最优学习率跨维度迁移，带来1.58倍计算效率提升，并保证训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数迁移规律主要针对一阶优化器，无法从根本上解决大规模训练的不稳定性问题。超球面优化方法通过将权重矩阵约束在固定范数超球面上，为更稳定的缩放提供了可能，但缺乏相应的学习率迁移理论。

Method: 提出HyperP（超球面参数化）框架，在Frobenius球约束和Muon优化器下实现最优学习率跨模型宽度、深度、训练词元数和MoE粒度的迁移。证明了权重衰减在Frobenius球上是一阶无效操作，Depth-μP仍然必要。提出SqrtGate MoE门控机制以保持不同MoE粒度下的输出RMS稳定。

Result: 单个基础学习率在最小规模上调优后可迁移至所有计算预算，在6×10²¹ FLOPs下相比Muon基线获得1.58倍计算效率提升。所有监控的不稳定性指标（Z值、输出RMS、激活异常值）在训练FLOPs扩展下保持有界且非递增。超球面优化允许更大的辅助负载均衡权重，实现强性能和良好的专家平衡。

Conclusion: HyperP成功解决了超球面优化下的超参数迁移问题，提供了可迁移的稳定性，为大语言模型的高效稳定缩放开辟了新路径。所提出的理论和实践方法为未来大规模模型训练提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws for large language models depend critically on the optimizer and parameterization. Existing hyperparameter transfer laws are mainly developed for first-order optimizers, and they do not structurally prevent training instability at scale. Recent hypersphere optimization methods constrain weight matrices to a fixed-norm hypersphere, offering a promising alternative for more stable scaling. We introduce HyperP (Hypersphere Parameterization), the first framework for transferring optimal learning rates across model width, depth, training tokens, and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) granularity under the Frobenius-sphere constraint with the Muon optimizer. We prove that weight decay is a first-order no-op on the Frobenius sphere, show that Depth-$μ$P remains necessary, and find that the optimal learning rate follows the same data-scaling power law with the "magic exponent" 0.32 previously observed for AdamW. A single base learning rate tuned at the smallest scale transfers across all compute budgets under HyperP, yielding $1.58\times$ compute efficiency over a strong Muon baseline at $6\times10^{21}$ FLOPs. Moreover, HyperP delivers transferable stability: all monitored instability indicators, including $Z$-values, output RMS, and activation outliers, remain bounded and non-increasing under training FLOPs scaling. We also propose SqrtGate, an MoE gating mechanism derived from the hypersphere constraint that preserves output RMS across MoE granularities for improved granularity scaling, and show that hypersphere optimization enables substantially larger auxiliary load-balancing weights, yielding both strong performance and good expert balance. We release our training codebase at https://github.com/microsoft/ArchScale.

</details>


### [263] [Stop Probing, Start Coding: Why Linear Probes and Sparse Autoencoders Fail at Compositional Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28744)
*Vitória Barin Pacela,Shruti Joshi,Isabela Camacho,Simon Lacoste-Julien,David Klindt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏自编码器（SAE）在组合偏移下失效的根本原因在于字典学习失败，而非分摊推理问题。通过对比实验证明，即使使用优化的推理过程，SAE学习到的字典方向仍然存在显著偏差，这为解决叠加表示下的稀疏推理指明了关键方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性表示假设认为神经网络激活将高级概念编码为线性混合，但在叠加场景下，这种编码是从高维概念空间到低维激活空间的投影，概念空间中的线性决策边界在投影后未必保持线性。经典稀疏编码方法通过每样本迭代推理利用压缩感知保证恢复潜在因子，而稀疏自编码器（SAE）将稀疏推理分摊到固定编码器中，引入了系统性差距。研究动机在于探究这一分摊差距在不同条件下的表现及其对OOD组合偏移的影响。

Method: 通过控制实验分解SAE的失败机制，系统地比较了SAE与经典稀疏编码方法在不同训练集规模、潜维度和稀疏水平下的表现。设计了Oracle基线方法，使用理想字典进行测试。将SAE编码器替换为基于相同字典的每样本FISTA算法，以隔离推理过程和字典学习的影响，从而识别出约束瓶颈所在。

Result: 分摊差距在所有测试的训练集规模、潜维度和稀疏水平中持续存在，导致SAE在OOD组合偏移下失效。关键发现是：SAE学习到的字典指向方向存在显著错误，而字典学习而非推理过程是关键约束。在相同字典上用每样本FISTA替换编码器无法消除差距。Oracle基线证明在所有测试规模下，使用好的字典可以解决问题。

Conclusion: 研究结果重新定义了SAE失败的本质：这是字典学习挑战，而非分摊问题。可扩展的字典学习是叠加表示下稀疏推理的关键开放性问题，为未来研究指明了方向。这一发现对理解和改进神经网络中的稀疏表示学习具有重要意义。

Abstract: The linear representation hypothesis states that neural network activations encode high-level concepts as linear mixtures. However, under superposition, this encoding is a projection from a higher-dimensional concept space into a lower-dimensional activation space, and a linear decision boundary in the concept space need not remain linear after projection. In this setting, classical sparse coding methods with per-sample iterative inference leverage compressed sensing guarantees to recover latent factors. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs), on the other hand, amortise sparse inference into a fixed encoder, introducing a systematic gap. We show this amortisation gap persists across training set sizes, latent dimensions, and sparsity levels, causing SAEs to fail under out-of-distribution (OOD) compositional shifts. Through controlled experiments that decompose the failure, we identify dictionary learning -- not the inference procedure -- as the binding constraint: SAE-learned dictionaries point in substantially wrong directions, and replacing the encoder with per-sample FISTA on the same dictionary does not close the gap. An oracle baseline proves the problem is solvable with a good dictionary at all scales tested. Our results reframe the SAE failure as a dictionary learning challenge, not an amortisation problem, and point to scalable dictionary learning as the key open problem for sparse inference under superposition.

</details>


### [264] [Temporal Credit Is Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28750)
*Aur Shalev Merin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用即时导数替代RTRL进行在线训练，通过梯度归一化在某些架构下达到全RTRL性能，内存减少1000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战RNN在线训练需RTRL及其高内存开销的传统认知，探讨即时导数在适当归一化下是否足够。

Method: 停用陈旧跟踪记忆，采用即时导数；提出架构规则：当梯度必须通过无输出旁路的非线性状态更新时，采用归一化（如RMSprop），否则无需归一化。

Result: 在十种架构、灵长类神经数据和流式ML基准上，即时导数+RMSprop匹敌或优于全RTRL，扩展至n=1024，内存降低约1000倍。

Conclusion: RNN在线训练无需雅可比传播；即时导数配合条件性归一化即可高效训练，显著节省内存并保持性能。

Abstract: Recurrent networks do not need Jacobian propagation to adapt online. The hidden state already carries temporal credit through the forward pass; immediate derivatives suffice if you stop corrupting them with stale trace memory and normalize gradient scales across parameter groups. An architectural rule predicts when normalization is needed: \b{eta}2 is required when gradients must pass through a nonlinear state update with no output bypass, and unnecessary otherwise. Across ten architectures, real primate neural data, and streaming ML benchmarks, immediate derivatives with RMSprop match or exceed full RTRL, scaling to n = 1024 at 1000x less memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Efficient CMOS Invertible Logic Using Stochastic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27030)
*Sean C. Smithson,Naoya Onizawa,Brett H. Meyer,Warren J. Gross,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机计算脉冲神经网络的可逆逻辑实现方法，设计出可用少量CMOS硬件实现的可逆随机逻辑门，并成功构建了可逆加法器和乘法器电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆逻辑能够在正向模式下产生正确输出，也能在反向模式下根据输出反推一致的输入，在解决因数分解和组合优化等挑战性问题上展现出优势。现有方法基于玻尔兹曼机构建，本文旨在探索更简洁高效的硬件实现方案。

Method: 基于随机计算原理，利用脉冲神经网络实现可逆逻辑；提出可逆随机逻辑门的设计方法，采用少量CMOS电路实现基本逻辑门；将基本门扩展构建可逆加法器和乘法器电路。

Result: 设计的可逆随机逻辑门能够正确实现基本逻辑门的功能并具备可逆性；成功构建了可逆加法器和乘法器电路；实验验证了综合电路能够正确执行乘法和因数分解操作；制作了可逆乘法器ASIC芯片并获得了实测结果。

Conclusion: 基于随机计算脉冲神经网络的可逆逻辑实现方法切实可行，能够用简洁的CMOS硬件实现复杂的可逆逻辑电路，为解决因数分解和组合优化等难题提供了新的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Invertible logic can operate in one of two modes: 1) a forward mode, in which inputs are presented and a single, correct output is produced, and 2) a reverse mode, in which the output is fixed and the inputs take on values consistent with the output. It is possible to create invertible logic using various Boltzmann machine configurations. Such systems have been shown to solve certain challenging problems quickly, such as factorization and combinatorial optimization. In this paper, we show that invertible logic can be implemented using simple spiking neural networks based on stochastic computing. We present a design methodology for invertible stochastic gates, which can be implemented using a small amount of CMOS hardware. We demonstrate that our design can not only correctly implement basic gates with invertible capability, but can also be extended to construct invertible stochastic adder and multiplier circuits. Experimental results are presented which demonstrate correct operation of synthesizable invertible circuitry performing both multiplication and factorization, along with fabricated ASIC measurement results for an invertible multiplier circuit.

</details>


### [266] [A 64-Spin All-to-All CMOS Ising Machine with Landscape Perturbation Achieving 2.28 nJ/Edge-Bit Energy-to-Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27402)
*Ahmet Yusuf Salim,Jianan Wu,Soner Seçkiner,Eslam Elmitwalli,Selçuk Köse,Zeljko Ignjatovic*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在65 nm CMOS工艺上实现了一个64自旋全对全电流耦合伊辛机，通过连续编程刷新机制缓解泄漏并提供确定性能量景观扰动，显著提升了解的质量和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊辛机在解决组合优化问题方面具有潜力，但现有实现面临电路泄漏、解质量不稳定等挑战。需要设计一种既能抑制泄漏又能提升求解质量的硬件架构。

Method: 采用65 nm CMOS工艺实现64自旋全对全电流模式耦合架构；设计连续编程刷新机制，既用于缓解泄漏电流，又作为确定性能量景观扰动的手段；支持31个系数级别，芯片面积为0.943 mm²。

Result: 实现了2.28 nJ/edge-bit的能效表现；与传统无景观扰动操作相比，连续编程刷新机制带来的确定性能量景观扰动能够一致地提升解的质量和成功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的电流模式耦合伊辛机架构结合连续编程刷新机制，有效解决了泄漏问题并提高了求解性能，证明了能量景观扰动对于改善伊辛机求解质量的重要性，为大规模优化问题的硬件加速提供了有效方案。

Abstract: A 64-spin all-to-all current-mode coupling Ising machine is implemented in 65 nm CMOS. The design supports 31 coefficient levels in 0.943 mm2 and achieves Energy-to-Solution (ETS) of 2.28 nJ/edge-bit. Continuous programming refresh not only mitigates leakage but also provides a mechanism for deterministic energy landscape perturbation, which consistently improves solution quality with higher success rate compared to operation without landscape perturbation.

</details>


### [267] [Expert Streaming: Accelerating Low-Batch MoE Inference via Multi-chiplet Architecture and Dynamic Expert Trajectory Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27624)
*Songchen Ma,Hongyi Li,Weihao Zhang,Yonghao Tan,Pingcheng Dong,Yu Liu,Lan Liu,Yuzhong Jiao,Xuejiao Liu,Luhong Liang,Kwang-Ting Cheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出FSE-DP，一种面向多芯粒加速器的MoE推理并行范式，通过细粒度调度实现计算通信重叠与负载均衡，显著提升性能并节省片上内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI场景下MoE推理面临片上内存受限、负载严重失衡及卸载带来的片外访存瓶颈问题，而高带宽芯粒互连为细粒度调度提供了新机遇。

Method: 提出全分片专家数据并行（FSE-DP），通过高带宽D2D链路沿动态轨迹编排细粒度互补专家流，并采用极简硬件友好的虚拟化规则与轻量级调度算法来管理数据流复杂性。

Result: 相比最先进基线实现1.22x至2.00x加速，并节省高达78.8%的片上内存。

Conclusion: FSE-DP有效解决了多芯粒系统上低批量MoE推理的关键瓶颈，为边缘AI部署提供了高效的并行化方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts is a promising approach for edge AI with low-batch inference. Yet, on-device deployments often face limited on-chip memory and severe workload imbalance; the prevalent use of offloading further incurs off-chip memory access bottlenecks. Moreover, MoE sparsity and dynamic gating shift distributed strategies toward much finer granularity and introduce runtime scheduling considerations. Recently, high die-to-die bandwidth chiplet interconnects have created new opportunities for multi-chiplet systems to address workload imbalance and offloading bottlenecks with fine-grained scheduling. In this paper, we propose Fully Sharded Expert Data Parallelism, a parallelization paradigm specifically architected for low-batch MoE inference on multi-chiplet accelerators. FSE-DP attains adaptive computation-communication overlap and balanced load by orchestrating fine-grained, complementary expert streams along dynamic trajectories across high-bandwidth D2D links. The attendant dataflow complexity is tamed by a minimal, hardware-amenable set of virtualization rules and a lightweight scheduling algorithm. Our approach achieves 1.22 to 2.00 times speedup over state-of-the-art baselines and saves up to 78.8 percent on-chip memory.

</details>


### [268] [RTLSeek: Boosting the LLM-Based RTL Generation with Multi-Stage Diversity-Oriented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27630)
*Xinyu Zhang,Zhiteng Chao,Yonghao Wang,Bin Sun,Tianyun Ma,Tianmeng Yang,Jianan Mu,Jing Justin Ye,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出RTLSeek，一种基于规则导向的多样性强化学习后训练范式，通过有限数据提升RTL生成的正确性与多样性，在RTLLM基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的RTL生成方法受限于高质量可验证数据的稀缺性，且后训练通常为每个规范生成单一HDL实现，缺乏对不同设计目标所需的RTL变体的感知。

Method: 提出RTLSeek后训练范式，采用基于规则的多样性导向强化学习，设计了多样性中心多目标奖励调度机制，整合专家知识与EDA工具反馈，并通过三阶段框架最大化有限数据的效用。

Result: 在RTLLM基准测试上，RTLSeek超越现有方法；消融实验证实，鼓励更广的设计空间探索能够提升RTL质量，实现了“生成越多，结果越好”的原则。

Conclusion: 通过在强化学习后训练中引入多样性导向策略与多目标奖励调度，可有效解决数据稀缺问题，同时提升RTL生成的正确性与多样性，为硬件设计自动化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Register Transfer Level (RTL) design translates high-level specifications into hardware using HDLs such as Verilog. Although LLM-based RTL generation is promising, the scarcity of functionally verifiable high-quality data limits both accuracy and diversity. Existing post-training typically produces a single HDL implementation per specification, lacking awareness of RTL variations needed for different design goals. We propose RTLSeek, a post-training paradigm that applies rule-based Diversity-Oriented Reinforcement Learning to improve RTL correctness and diversity. Our Diversity-Centric Multi-Objective Reward Scheduling integrates expert knowledge with EDA feedback, and a three-stage framework maximizes the utility of limited data. Experiments on the RTLLM benchmark show that RTLSeek surpasses prior methods, with ablation results confirming that encouraging broader design-space exploration improves RTL quality and achieves the principle of "the more generated, the better results." Implementation framework, including the dataset, source code, and model weights, is shown at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DAC2026ID71-ACB4/.

</details>


### [269] [MCPT-Solver: An Monte Carlo Algorithm Solver Using MTJ Devices for Particle Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28042)
*Siqing Fu,Lizhou Wu,Tiejun Li,Xuchao Xie,Chunyuan Zhang,Sheng Ma,Jianmin Zhang,Yuhan Tang,Jixuan Tang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出MCPT-Solver，一种基于自旋器件的可调概率真随机数生成器，用于高效求解蒙特卡洛粒子输运问题，在精度、速度和能效方面均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛粒子输运问题在科学计算中至关重要，但在传统冯·诺依曼架构上求解时，随机分支和不规则内存访问导致计算效率低下，根本原因在于随机算法与确定性硬件之间的不匹配。

Method: 利用自旋器件的随机切换特性作为高质量熵源构建真随机数生成器（TRNG），通过优化控制逻辑和写入机制实现高吞吐量和工艺-电压-温度（PVT）容差；设计硬件贝叶斯推理网络实现可调概率的随机数输出；构建系统级仿真框架进行评估。

Result: MCPT-Solver求解输运问题的均方误差为7.6e-6，相比通用处理器显著加速；吞吐量达185 Mb/s，面积开销为27.8 um2/bit，能耗为8.6 pJ/bit，是首个兼具PVT容差和可调概率特性的自旋基TRNG。

Conclusion: MCPT-Solver成功弥合了随机算法与确定性硬件之间的差距，为蒙特卡洛粒子输运等随机应用提供了一种高效、精确且硬件友好的解决方案，展示了自旋器件在随机计算领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Monte Carlo particle transport problems play a vital role in scientific computing, but solving them on exiting von Neumann architectures suffers from random branching and irregular memory access, causing computing inefficiency due to a fundamental mismatch between stochastic algorithms and deterministic hardware. To bridge this gap, we propose MCPT-Solver, a spin-based hardware true random number generator (TRNG) with tunable output probability enabled by a Bayesian inference network architecture. It is dedicated for efficiently solving stochastic applications including Monte Carlo particle transport problems. First, we leverage the stochastic switching property of spin devices to provide a high-quality entropy source for the TRNG and achieve high generating throughput and process-voltage-temperature tolerance through optimized control logic and write mechanism designs. Next, we propose a hardware Bayesian inference network to enable probability-tunable random number outputs. Finally, we present a system-level simulation framework to evaluate MCPT-Solver. Experimental results show that MCPT-Solver achieves a mean squared error of 7.6e-6 for solving transport problems while demonstrating a dramatic acceleration effect over general-purpose processors. Additionally, the MCPT-Solver's throughput reaches 185 Mb/s with an area of 27.8 um2/bit and energy consumption of 8.6 pJ/bit, making it the first spin-based TRNG that offers both process-voltage-temperature tolerance and adjustable probability.

</details>


### [270] [AXON: An Automated Netlist Optimization Framework for High-Speed Adders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28184)
*Tiantian Yang,Xuanle Ren,Qingdian Wan,Qi Meng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出AXON自动化加法器网表优化框架，通过架构级到网表级的设计空间探索和标准单元感知映射，实现了相比商业综合工具最高10.3%延迟降低、12.6%面积-延迟积改善和32.1%能耗-延迟积改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有加法器设计方法缺乏网表级和单元级感知，通用综合启发式算法并非针对加法器优化，导致无法在特定设计目标下实现全局最优的性能、功耗和面积（PPA）权衡。

Method: 提出AXON框架，采用分层方法将前缀拓扑搜索与标准单元感知映射相结合，实现从架构级到网表级的设计空间探索；同时引入混合超高速加法器架构，结合并行前缀和Ling架构以缩短关键路径。

Result: 在TSMC 28nm工艺库上实验验证，AXON相比商业综合工具在延迟、面积-延迟积和能耗-延迟积方面分别提升高达10.3%、12.6%和32.1%。

Conclusion: AXON框架通过集成架构级拓扑搜索和网表级单元感知映射，能够快速收敛到近优PPA解决方案，为加法器设计提供了一种有效的自动化优化方法。

Abstract: Adders are fundamental building blocks in modern digital systems, and their performance, power, and area (PPA) directly impact system efficiency. Contemporary adders typically use parallel-prefix architectures with established PPA trade-offs, but these often fail to deliver globally optimal PPA for specific design goals. Prior work lacks netlist-/cell-level awareness, and general synthesis heuristics are not adder-specific, resulting in suboptimal PPA. To address this, we propose AXON, an automated netlist optimization framework for adders. It performs design space exploration from architectural to netlist level, integrating prefix topology search with standard-cell-aware mapping via a hierarchical approach to quickly converge to near-optimal PPA solutions. We also introduce a hybrid ultra-high-speed adder combining parallel-prefix and Ling architectures to shorten the critical path. Experiments on TSMC 28nm library show AXON improves delay, area-delay product, and energy-delay product by up to 10.3%, 12.6%, and 32.1% respectively, compared to commercial synthesis tools.

</details>


### [271] [A Switch-Centric In-Network Architecture for Accelerating LLM Inference in Shared-Memory Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28239)
*Aojie Jiang,Kang Zhu,Zhiheng Zhang,Zhengxu Su,Juntao Liu,Yuan Du,Li Du*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出SCIN，首个面向AI加速器共享内存网络的交换中心化网内计算架构，通过交换机内加速器(ISA)消除冗余数据移动并支持网内量化(INQ)，实现了比NVLS更低的All-Reduce延迟和近两倍的带宽提升，在LLaMA-2模型上TTFT提升高达1.74倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网内计算技术(如NVLink Sharp)采用加速器中心化架构存在两大根本局限：1)依赖GPU加载指令触发归约操作，导致归约后的数据必须额外传回发起GPU而非直接广播，引入不必要的通信开销；2)受架构限制无法卸载不可分解为内存语义指令的操作符(如网内量化)，导致All-Reduce必须以FP16/BF16精度运行，造成大量带宽浪费。

Method: 提出SCIN交换中心化网内架构：1)设计交换机内加速器(ISA)，能够主动发起内存语义操作进行网内处理；2)协同设计通信架构，引入可忽略的协议开销；3)在ISA中集成量化模块，实现All-Reduce的网内量化(INQ)，将精度降至8位；4)在多FPGA系统上构建原型验证可行性和有效性。

Result: SCIN相比NVLS实现了更低的All-Reduce延迟；通过INQ将All-Reduce精度降至8位，在精度损失可忽略的情况下将近乎翻倍带宽；实验结果显示All-Reduce加速比最高达8.7倍(小消息)和3.8倍(大消息)，在LLaMA-2模型上TTFT提升高达1.74倍，TPOT提升1.34倍。

Conclusion: SCIN作为首个交换中心化网内计算架构，通过消除冗余数据移动和支持网内量化，有效解决了LLM推理中的通信瓶颈问题，为AI加速器共享内存网络提供了低延迟、高带宽的All-Reduce解决方案，具有实际部署的可行性和显著的性能优势。

Abstract: In-network computing techniques, exemplified by NVLink Sharp (NVLS), offer a promising approach to addressing the communication bottlenecks in LLM inference by offloading collective operations, such as All-Reduce, to switches. However, the accelerator-centric architecture of NVLS suffers from two fundamental limitations: 1) it relies on GPU load instructions to trigger reduction operations, which means that the data reduced in the switch must be additionally transferred back to the initiating GPU rather than being broadcast directly, thereby introducing unnecessary communication overhead; 2) due to its architectural constraints, NVLS cannot offload operators that are not decomposable into memory-semantic instructions, such as the in-network quantization (INQ) proposed in this work. As a result, All-Reduce in NVLS must operate at FP16/BF16 precision, leading to substantial bandwidth waste.To address these limitations, we propose SCIN, the first switch-centric in-network architecture for shared-memory networks of AI accelerators, enabling both low-latency and high-bandwidth All-Reduce. Specifically, we introduce an in-switch accelerator (ISA) capable of initiating memory-semantic operations for in-network processing, together with a co-designed communication fabric that incurs negligible protocol overhead. By eliminating redundant data movement, SCIN delivers lower All-Reduce latency than NVLS. Moreover, by integrating a quantization module into the ISA, SCIN enables INQ for All-Reduce, reducing its precision to 8 bits and nearly doubling bandwidth with negligible accuracy loss. We also present a prototype of SCIN on a multi-FPGA system to demonstrate its feasibility and effectiveness. Experimental results show that our design accelerates All-Reduce by up to 8.7x for small messages and 3.8x for large messages, leading up to 1.74x faster TTFT and 1.34x faster TPOT on LLaMA-2 models.

</details>


### [272] [AceleradorSNN: A Neuromorphic Cognitive System Integrating Spiking Neural Networks and DynamicImage Signal Processing on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28429)
*Daniel Gutierrez,Ruben Martinez,Leyre Arnedo,Antonio Cuesta,Soukaina El Hamry*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AceleradorSNN，一种面向自动驾驶系统、无人机和工业4.0机器人的第三代AI认知系统，通过结合基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的神经形态处理单元和可重构认知ISP，在FPGA上实现了高速、低延迟且高效能的目标检测硬件架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统(ADAS)、无人机(UAV)和工业4.0机器人等自主系统对目标检测提出了高速、低延迟和高能效的严苛需求，传统卷积神经网络(CNN)在处理速度、延迟和能耗方面存在固有局限，难以满足实时性和能效要求。

Method: 设计了AceleradorSNN第三代AI认知系统架构，包含两个核心IP：(1)基于脉冲神经网络(SNN)的神经形态处理单元(NPU)，用于处理来自动态视觉传感器(DVS)的异步数据；(2)动态可重构认知图像信号处理器(ISP)用于RGB相机；采用替代梯度训练的SNN骨干网络，并在现场可编程门阵列(FPGA)上实现实时流式ISP架构。

Result: 完成了硬件导向的两个IP核心设计，成功评估了替代梯度训练的SNN骨干网络性能，并在FPGA平台上实现了实时流式ISP架构，验证了系统设计的可行性。

Conclusion: AceleradorSNN系统成功整合了SNN神经形态处理和传统RGB图像处理能力，为自主系统中的高速、低延迟、高能效目标检测提供了有效的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: The demand for high-speed, low-latency, and energy-efficient object detection in autonomous systems -- such as advanced driver-assistance systems (ADAS), unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), and Industry 4.0 robotics -- has exposed the limitations of traditional Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). To address these challenges, we have developed AceleradorSNN, a third-generation artificial intelligence cognitive system. This architecture integrates a Neuromorphic Processing Unit (NPU) based on Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) to process asynchronous data from Dynamic Vision Sensors (DVS), alongside a dynamically reconfigurable Cognitive Image Signal Processor (ISP) for RGB cameras. This paper details the hardware-oriented design of both IP cores, the evaluation of surrogate-gradienttrained SNN backbones, and the real-time streaming ISP architecture implemented on Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGA).

</details>


### [273] [Loop Control Management in Tightly Coupled Processor Arrays (TCPAs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28645)
*Dominik Walter,Frank Hannig,Jürgen Teich*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从多面体表示中推导并大幅精简控制条件的方法，实现了控制信号数量15x到45x的削减，并设计了一种轻量级全局控制器架构，在占用不到10%阵列资源的情况下实现了零开销的循环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维循环内核在并行循环加速器上执行时，控制开销往往占据主导地位。尽管紧耦合处理器阵列（TCPAs）将循环控制卸载到全局控制器（GC），但现有方法仍需数百个控制信号，开销巨大。

Method: 提出一种方法从迭代空间的多面体表示中推导并积极精简控制条件；引入一种轻量级GC架构，利用有界评估单元将条件评估为多面体的并集；通过最少数量的延迟元件在阵列中分发控制信号。

Result: 在多个基准测试中实现了控制信号数量15x到45x的削减；整个控制流仅需不到10%的阵列资源；硬件需求与单个处理元件相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功实现了零开销的循环控制，显著降低了控制信号的数量和硬件资源占用，为TCPAs上的多维循环执行提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multidimensional loop kernels often suffer from control overhead that can dominate execution time on parallel loop accelerators. Tightly Coupled Processor Arrays (TCPAs) offload loop control to a global controller (GC), but existing approaches still require hundreds of control signals. We propose a method to derive and aggressively reduce these control conditions from a polyhedral representation of the iteration space, achieving reductions of 15x to 45x in control signals across several benchmarks. We introduce a lightweight GC architecture that evaluates conditions as unions of polyhedra using bounded evaluation units, requiring hardware comparable to a single processing element. Control signals are distributed throughout the array with a minimal number of delay elements resulting in zero-overhead loop control. Our evaluation on PolyBench kernels shows that the entire control flow requires < 10 % of the total array resources.

</details>


### [274] [Physical Design of UET-RVMCU: A Streamlined Open-Source RISC-V Microcontroller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28709)
*Abdullah Azhar,Uneeb Kamal,Wajid Ali,Saad Gillani,Dr Suleman Sami Qazi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文基于开源的应用级RISC-V处理器UETRV-PCore，通过简化流水线、移除MMU并集成GPIO外设，设计并实现了轻量级微控制器UET-RVMCU，最终使用OpenLane开源流程成功完成了物理实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了创建一个易于获取且灵活的开源RISC-V微控制器，并证明将应用级SoC转换为适用于嵌入式系统的功能丰富型微控制器的可行性。

Method: 基于UETRV-PCore进行简化设计：减少流水线级数、移除存储管理单元（MMU）功能、集成通用输入输出（GPIO）外设；使用开源的RTL-to-GDS流程（OpenLane）生成最终GDSII版图。

Result: 成功设计并完成了UET-RVMCU的物理实现，生成最终GDSII布局，验证了开源RTL-to-GDS流程的可用性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将应用级SoC转化为面积小、设计简单的嵌入式微控制器是可行的，强调了开源开发在RISC-V生态中的价值。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and physical implementation of UET-RVMCU, a lightweight RISC-V microcontroller derived from the UETRV-PCore. Aimed at creating an accessible and flexible open-source RISC-V-based microcontroller, UET-RVMCU simplifies the application-class UETRV-PCore by reducing pipeline stages, removing MMU functionality, and integrating GPIO peripherals. The final GDSII layout was generated using an open-source RTL-to-GDS flow (OpenLane). This project demonstrates the feasibility of transforming an application-class SoC into a feature-rich microcontroller suitable for embedded systems, emphasizing low area, design simplicity, and open-source development.

</details>
